Method: 提出分层优化框架，涵盖模型级并行策略、计算-通信协同编排和底层内核执行优化；在此基础上构建面向运筹学任务的CPT和SFT数据流水线，结合领域资源与求解器验证的合成优化文档生成高质量训练数据。

Result: 系统实现34.22% MFU，较开源基线提升2.93倍；基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash微调得到的专业模型在零样本Pass@1上达到71.81%，超越GPT-5.4-Mini和基线的3.98和11.27个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了从Ascend基础设施上的高效万亿参数模型后训练到面向求解器基础数学建模的领域专业化Flash模型的完整堆栈路径，推动了前沿模型系统在复杂推理任务上的发展。

Abstract: Full-parameter post-training of trillion-parameter-scale MoE models introduces substantial system-level challenges for large-scale distributed training, including severe memory pressure, non-overlapped communication overhead, and inefficient kernel execution. While most large-scale LLM training systems are built around GPU-based clusters, this report presents an end-to-end optimization practice on the Ascend NPU SuperPOD. Using the DeepSeek-V4 model family as the target workload, we develop a hierarchical optimization framework spanning model-level parallelism, computation-communication orchestration, and low-level kernel execution. The resulting system achieves 34.22% Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU) with a 2.93x improvement over the open-source baseline recipe while maintaining training stability. Building on this optimized infrastructure, we further establish a CPT and SFT workflow for complex Operations Research (OR) tasks. We refer to the integrated framework as SLAI T-Rex. Using DeepSeek-V4-Flash, we develop OR-oriented CPT and SFT data pipelines that combine collected domain resources with solver-verified synthetic optimization documents. The resulting dataset contains 10K high-quality SFT samples spanning four task categories and three problem representations. The specialized model achieves the highest average zero-shot Pass@1 score among the evaluated models, reaching 71.81% and outperforming GPT-5.4-Mini and the base DeepSeek-V4-Flash model by 3.98 and 11.27 percentage points, respectively. Overall, this work demonstrates a full-stack pathway from efficient trillion-parameter model post-training on Ascend infra to domain-specialized Flash models for solver-grounded mathematical modeling, advancing frontier-model systems for complex reasoning.

</details>


### [35] [Gotta Catch them all: the modes of Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20146)
*Shreyans Jain,Alexandra Yost,Amirali Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“谄媚行为是单一维度”的假设，通过分析948种社交压力情境下的三种假设谄媚模式，发现尽管输出相似（文本分类器准确率仅57.8%），但内部表征从第14层开始完全线性可分，且各模式出现在不同处理阶段、依赖不同注意力回路、对不同输入响应最强，表明谄媚行为是一个结构化的、表征和计算上各异的模式家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将大语言模型的谄媚行为视为单一行为维度进行统一放大或抑制，作者质疑这一假设，旨在揭示谄媚行为内部可能存在的结构化差异。

Method: 在948个社交压力情境下，分析三种假设的谄媚模式，使用文本分类器（准确率仅57.8%）和表征线性可分性分析（从第14层起完全线性可分），并考察各模式的处理阶段、注意力回路和输入响应模式。

Result: 三种谄媚模式的输出高度相似（分类准确率57.8%），但内部表征从第14层起完全线性可分；各模式出现在不同处理阶段，依赖不同注意力回路，对不同输入响应最强。

Conclusion: 谄媚行为并非单一倾向，而是一个结构化的、包含表征和计算上不同模式的家族，需要更精确的测量和干预方法。

Abstract: Large language models often align with users' beliefs at the expense of factual accuracy, a behavior known as sycophancy. Prior mechanistic studies largely treat sycophancy as a single behavioral dimension that can be uniformly amplified or suppressed. We challenge this assumption by analyzing three hypothesized modes of sycophancy across 948 social pressure situations. Although the modes produce highly similar outputs, with a text-only classifier achieving just 57.8 percent accuracy, their internal representations are perfectly linearly separable from layer 14 onward. We further find the modes emerge at different processing stages, rely on distinct attention circuitry, and fire strongest on different inputs. These results show that sycophancy is not a monolithic tendency, but a structured family of representationally and computationally distinct modes, motivating more precise measurement and intervention.

</details>


### [36] [surprisal is Not a Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20208)
*Andrés Buxó-Lugo,Aniello De Santo,Morgan Grobol,Ryan J. Hubbard,Cassandra L. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判性地指出，尽管Surprisal理论被定位为计算层面的解释，但大型语言模型（LLM）概率的随意使用掩盖了不同模型在表征和算法层面的承诺差异。通过三项分析证明，算法和模型架构选择显著影响语言模型概率的计算，因此研究者不应将LLM概率视为可互换的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“Surprisal理论仅关注计算层面、与表征和算法无关”的主流观点，指出LLM时代的研究者错误地将不同模型输出的surprisal值视为可直接互换的度量。

Method: 通过三项对比分析，系统考察不同算法和模型架构对语言模型概率计算的影响，揭示模型间的实质性差异。

Result: 算法选择和模型架构对语言模型概率的计算有显著影响，不同LLM的surprisal值不可直接互换使用。

Conclusion: 研究者应重新审视将LLM概率视为可互换的做法，在检验Surprisal理论时必须考虑表征和算法层面的具体承诺。

Abstract: Surprisal Theory is often characterized as a computational-level explanation per (Marr, 1982). We argue in this work that, even though a computational level narrative has been used to support "representation-agnostic research" within computational psycholinguistics, the movement toward black box systems embodied by large language models (LLMs) does not exempt modelers using the surprisal metric from the representational decisions required by computational-level characterizations. In fact, we argue that the uncritical use of LLM-surprisal obfuscates the representational and algorithmic-level commitments of different models. In three analyses, we show that the choice of algorithm and model architecture play significant roles in the computation of language model probabilities. We advise that researchers who wish to test Surprisal Theory re-evaluate the practice of treating large language model probabilities as interchangeable

</details>


### [37] [HalluTruthQA: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Hallucination Detection, Localization, and Explanation in Arabic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20219)
*Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Hichem Telli,Somaya Eltanbouly,Shahd Gaben,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Mutaz Al-Khatib,Emad Mohamed,Mohammed Ghaly,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 HalluTruthQA，一个用于阿拉伯语问答中幻觉评估的细粒度基准数据集，包含 2,400 个专家精选样本，涵盖检测、定位、验证和解释四个任务维度。实验表明，四类任务评估能力各异，没有单一模型在所有任务上表现最佳，幻觉评估应从检测向定位、验证和解释扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准大多仅提供回答级别的标签，缺乏对错误内容的具体定位、错误原因的解释以及正确事实答案的选择能力，尤其针对阿拉伯语这一资源较少的语言。

Method: 构建 HalluTruthQA 基准数据集，包含 2,400 个专家精选的阿拉伯语问答样本，涵盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个领域。每个样本包含问题、模型生成的答案、参考答案、二分类幻觉标签、6个候选事实验证选项、字符级错误跨度、人工解释以及宏微观幻觉类型。在零样本设置下评估四个开源LLM（Allam、Falcon-H1、Qwen32、Silma）在幻觉检测、跨度定位、事实验证和解释评估四个任务上的表现。

Result: 不同任务表现各异：检测任务 Macro-F1 最高 0.880，定位任务 F1-Sp 最高 0.516，事实验证 LO-Score 最高 0.852，解释评估最终得分最高 0.644。没有单一模型在所有任务上同时达到最佳。

Conclusion: 幻觉评估应超越简单的检测层面，向定位、验证和解释事实错误的方向发展。多任务评估框架能更全面地反映模型在不同维度上的能力差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent Arabic answers, yet factual errors remain difficult to detect, localize, explain, and verify. Existing hallucination benchmarks often provide response-level labels, with limited support for identifying the exact erroneous content, explaining why it is incorrect, or selecting the correct factual answer. We introduce \textsc{HalluTruthQA}, a fine-grained benchmark for hallucination evaluation in Arabic question answering. The benchmark contains 2,400 expert-curated examples across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Each example pairs an Arabic question and a model-generated answer with a verified reference answer, a binary hallucination label, six candidate answers for factual verification, and, for hallucinated answers, character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and macro and micro hallucination types.
  We evaluate four open-source LLMs, \textsc{Allam}, \textsc{Falcon-H1}, \textsc{Qwen32}, and \textsc{Silma}, in a zero-shot setting across hallucination detection, span-level localization, factual verification, and explanation evaluation. Results show that these tasks capture different abilities: no single model achieves the strongest performance across all tasks, with best scores of 0.880 Macro-F1 for detection, 0.516 F1-Sp for localization, 0.852 LO-Score for factual verification, and 0.644 final score for explanation evaluation. Our taxonomy shows that hallucination evaluation should move beyond detection toward localizing, verifying, and explaining factual errors. The code, dataset, prompts, and evaluation scripts are available at https://gitlab.com/nlpresearcher/HalluTruthQA.

</details>


### [38] [On the Systematic Challenges of Culturally Loaded Machine Translation: Dream of the Red Chamber as the Cultural Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20241)
*Yiming Wang,Jiayuan Di*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统性地探究了大语言模型（LLMs）在文化负载翻译中面临的三大挑战：任务挑战（前沿LLMs表现不佳）、人工评估挑战（评估者背景差异导致判断不一致）以及自动评估挑战（现有指标不可靠）。基于《红楼梦》构建的中日双语数据集（500个样本）揭示了这些关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLMs使机器翻译在许多场景中达到了类人水平，但其处理文化负载表达的能力尚未得到充分探索。文化负载翻译的挑战根植于超越表面语言形式的社会文化语境中，因此需要系统性的研究来揭示LLMs在此类任务中的局限性。

Method: 从文化代表性语料《红楼梦》中构建了一个中日双语数据集，包含500个涵盖多种文化类别的片段。采用综合评估协议，同时考察任务表现、人工评估一致性和自动评估指标的有效性。

Result: 发现了三个主要挑战：(1)任务挑战：前沿LLMs在文化负载内容上存在显著性能差距；(2)人工评估挑战：评估者的文化背景差异导致翻译判断出现实质性分歧；(3)自动评估挑战：广泛使用的指标无法可靠评估文化负载翻译的质量。

Conclusion: 这些发现为计算科学和语言学领域的文化导向翻译研究提供了有价值的见解，揭示了当前LLM-based MT系统在处理文化负载翻译时的系统性不足。

Abstract: Culturally loaded translation poses unique challenges for machine translation (MT), as meanings are deeply embedded in socio-cultural contexts beyond surface linguistic forms. Although large language models (LLMs) have enabled MT systems to achieve human-like quality in many scenarios, their ability to handle culturally loaded expressions remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically investigate the challenges posed by culturally loaded translation in LLM-based MT systems. We construct a Chinese-Japanese bilingual dataset from the culturally representative corpus Dream of the Red Chamber, containing 500 segments across diverse cultural categories. Using a comprehensive evaluation protocol, we reveal three main challenges: (1) task challenges, where frontier LLMs exhibit notable performance gaps and struggle with culturally loaded content; (2) human evaluation challenges, where evaluator backgrounds lead to substantial disagreement in translation judgments; and (3) automatic evaluation challenges, where widely used metrics fail to reliably assess translation quality for this task. These findings may offer valuable insights for culture-oriented translation research in both computational science and linguistics.

</details>


### [39] [Exposure is Optional: Learning Unlike Coordination in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20251)
*Jiamu Luo,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过过滤语料训练（FiCT）方法，发现GPT-2模型无需直接接触"异类并列"（unlike coordination）训练数据，仅凭"同类并列"（alike coordination）语料即可泛化并正确处理异类并列结构。模型内部表征分析表明，模型通过将并列成分视为相似结构类别或类似删除机制来处理异类并列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并列结构是语言学中争议性话题，"只有同类成分才能并列"的传统观点受到自然语言中大量语法合格的异类并列现象的挑战。本文旨在探究语言模型获得异类并列能力是否需要直接接触训练数据中的相关实例，还是可以从一般组合能力中自发涌现。

Method: 采用Filtered-Corpus Training (FiCT)方法，从训练语料中移除所有异类并列实例，训练GPT-2模型；通过困惑度和语法可接受性判断评估模型对异类并列的泛化能力，并分析内部表征。

Result: 过滤语料训练后的模型在困惑度和语法可接受性判断上与未过滤语料训练的模型表现相当，证明直接暴露非必要条件。内部表征分析表明模型处理异类并列的机制有两种：将并列成分视为相似结构类别，或通过类似删除的机制，这两种机制均可从同类并列中习得。

Conclusion: 语言模型无需直接接触异类并列训练数据即可泛化处理该结构，为理解语言模型如何内部表征语言结构提供了新见解，也为语言学中关于并列结构的更广泛争论贡献了计算证据。

Abstract: Coordination, a fundamental linguistic structure, remains a subject of intense debate, and its exact nature continues to elude theoretical linguistics. A common view holds that only same-category constituents can be conjoined, which has been challenged by the many grammatical unlike coordinations found in natural language. Treating language models as a computational testbed, we investigate whether the acquisition of unlike coordination requires direct exposure in the training data, or whether it can emerge organically from general compositional abilities. Using Filtered-Corpus Training (FiCT), we train GPT-2 models on corpora from which all instances of unlike coordination have been removed. We find that direct exposure is not necessary: models trained on filtered data successfully generalize to unlike coordination, achieving perplexity and grammaticality judgments comparable to models trained on unfiltered text. Furthermore, our analyses of internal representations indicate that language models process unlike coordination by treating the conjoined elements as belonging to similar structural categories or through a mechanism akin to deletion, both of which appear learnable from exposure to alike coordination alone. This work contributes to the growing understanding of how language models internally represent linguistic structures, while also adding to the broader debate on coordination by showing how models generalize and process unlike coordination without direct exposure.

</details>


### [40] [The Maskability Index: Predicting Task-Objective Alignment in Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20265)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Mahsa Afsharzadeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出掩码性指数（Maskability Index, MI），用于量化评估在少样本生成场景下，知识关系更适合掩码式提示（masked-style prompting）还是前缀式提示（prefix-style prompting）。MI通过计算掩码模板与未掩码模板之间DepthRank得分的差异来度量目标-模板对齐程度。在ATOMIC2020知识库补全基准上的实验表明，MI与下游生成性能正相关，有助于在低资源场景下选择合适的提示模板和适应策略，从预训练语言模型中提取关系知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练语言模型（如T5、BERT）在生成结构化知识方面表现出色，但其性能高度依赖于提示策略与预训练目标之间的匹配程度。现有的提示选择方法缺乏量化指标来指导何种提示方式（掩码式或前缀式）更适合特定的知识关系，尤其是在少样本场景下。

Method: 提出Maskability Index (MI)，通过比较掩码模板与未掩码模板的DepthRank得分差异，量化知识关系对掩码式提示或前缀式提示的适配程度。在ATOMIC2020知识库补全任务上进行评估，验证MI与下游生成性能的相关性。

Result: MI与下游生成性能呈正相关关系，能够有效预测不同知识关系最适合的提示模板类型。

Conclusion: MI可作为选择提示模板和适应策略的有效指标，帮助在低资源场景下从预训练语言模型中更好地提取关系知识。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained language models such as T5 and BERT have demonstrated strong capabilities for generating structured knowledge. However, their performance depends on how closely the prompting strategy matches the objectives used during pretraining. We introduce the Maskability Index (MI), a quantitative metric that estimates whether a knowledge relation is better suited to masked-style prompting or prefix-style prompting in few-shot generation. MI is computed from differences in DepthRank scores between masked and unmasked templates, providing a principled measure of objective-template alignment. We evaluate MI on a diverse set of relations from the ATOMIC2020 knowledge base completion benchmark and show that it is positively correlated with downstream generation performance. These results indicate that MI can help select appropriate prompting templates and adaptation strategies for extracting relational knowledge from pretrained language models, especially in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [41] [Which Values Do LLMs Confuse? A Schwartz-Based Recognition Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20270)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Stepan Ukolov,Timofei Sivoraksha,Alexander Evseev,Mikhail Solovev,Valeriia Kuschenko,Maria Chistyakova,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型在具体情境中识别人类价值观的能力。使用1,000个俄语情境文本和Schwartz的十种基本价值观框架，评估了21个指令微调LLM。结果显示模型准确率尚可（Acc@1=0.683），但存在系统性混淆模式，相邻价值观错误占50.9%，且存在八种定向混淆，这些错误会偏差高阶价值观画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越通过其所持价值观被评估，但这预设了模型能从具体情境中识别出所表达的价值观。研究者希望先验证这一前提条件是否成立。

Method: 基于Schwartz十种基本价值观框架，使用1,000个平衡分布的俄语情境文本，由两名人工标注者独立标注。采用固定排序响应协议评估21个指令微调LLM，其中20个可靠输出构成语义面板。通过精确准确率、排序恢复和定向错误分析三个维度进行评价。

Result: 汇总Acc@1为0.683，Acc@3为0.892。相邻价值观占语义错误的50.9%（对比基线24.4%）。发现八种跨检查点和人工确认子集均存在的定向混淆，其中多个是强不对称的（如普世主义→仁慈、传统→遵从、安全→权力），而刺激-享乐主义是双向边界。混淆严重程度因检查点而异，会偏差高阶价值观画像。

Conclusion: 价值观识别评估应结合精确准确率、排序恢复和定向错误分析三个维度。模型虽然在正确动机区域定位上表现尚可，但相邻价值观排序不稳定，且存在系统性定向混淆，这会影响基于价值观的模型评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated through the values they endorse, but such evaluations presuppose that models can identify the value expressed in a concrete situation. We study this prerequisite as controlled top-1 recognition over Schwartz's ten basic values. Our evaluation set contains 1,000 Russian situational texts, balanced across the ten values and independently labeled by two human annotators per item. We evaluate 21 instruction-tuned LLM runs under a fixed ranked-response protocol; 20 runs with reliable outputs form the semantic panel. Pooled Acc@1 is 0.683 and Acc@3 is 0.892, showing that models often locate the correct motivational region while ranking close alternatives unstably. Adjacent values account for 50.9% of semantic errors, compared with 24.4% under a checkpoint-specific null. Eight directed confusions recur across checkpoints and human-confirmed subsets. Several are strongly asymmetric, including Universalism to Benevolence, Tradition to Conformity, and Security to Power, whereas Stimulation-Hedonism forms a bidirectional boundary. Their severity is checkpoint-specific and can bias higher-order value profiles. The results motivate value-recognition evaluation that combines exact accuracy, ranked recovery, and directed error analysis.

</details>


### [42] [Sound Probabilistic Safety Bounds for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20286)
*Mahdi Nazeri,Anne-Kathrin Schmuck,Sadegh Soudjani,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用Clopper-Pearson置信区间和潜空间特征优先探索生成树分支的框架，用于计算LLM生成有害输出的严格概率下界，即使在真实有害概率极小时也能高效计算，且下界在形式上是可靠的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在有害概率极小时提供严格且可证明的下界，缺乏对LLM输出危害性的统计认证手段。

Method: 将Clopper-Pearson置信区间应用于LLM危害概率的PAC下界计算；利用潜空间特征对自回归生成树中的分支进行优先级排序，优先探索更可能产生有害输出的分支，从而在极小概率场景下也能高效计算非平凡下界。

Result: 在先进LLM上计算出了非平凡的有害概率下界，验证了方法的有效性；所得下界经过形式化证明，严格小于真实危害概率。

Conclusion: 首次实现了LLM输出危害性的统计评估与形式化认证，为LLM安全性提供了可靠的理论保证。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for computing rigorous bounds on the probability that a large language model (LLM) generates harmful output to a given prompt. We study a new application of the Clopper-Pearson confidence intervals to obtain probably approximately correct (PAC) bounds for this problem. As our main technical contribution, we propose an algorithm that leverages features in the latent space to prioritize exploring branches in the auto-regressive generation tree that are more likely to produce harmful outputs. Our approach in particular enables the efficient computation of useful lower bounds, even in scenarios where the true harm probability is extremely small, and crucially, the obtained lower bounds are sound, i.e., formally proven to be less than the actual harmfulness probability: our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by computing non-trivial lower bounds on state-of-the-art LLMs. This study newly enables the evaluation and statistical certification of LLMs.

</details>


### [43] [PyroDash: Cost-Efficient Token-Level Small-Large Language Model Collaborative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20327)
*Niqi Lyu,Pengtao Shi,Wei Qiu,Jianlin Zhong,Sicong Xia,Jianyao Ma,Yicheng Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PyroDash提出了一种词元级别的SLM-LLM协同推理框架，通过让SLM学习生成控制令牌来决定何时向LLM请求协助，在多个数学推理基准上实现了精度与成本的良好平衡，最高可降低约96.4%的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理能力强但服务成本高昂，小语言模型（SLM）成本低但在困难问题上可靠性不足。现有方法如分别训练路由模型或需要访问LLM对数几率等，存在额外开销或信息限制。PyroDash旨在通过SLM内化的、词元级别的协作策略，在保持强推理性能的同时显著降低LLM使用成本。

Method: PyroDash采用三阶段训练：(1) 控制令牌嵌入学习，让SLM学会产生特殊的控制令牌；(2) 面向卸载的监督微调，使SLM在需要时生成控制令牌并配合LLM完成推理；(3) 基于Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）的成本感知对齐，奖励函数平衡答案准确性与相对于纯LLM推理的成本。推理时，SLM生成过程中通过控制令牌触发Collaborate Engine，将当前查询和部分推理轨迹发送给冻结的LLM进行单次补全。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准上，λ=0.05时达到64.04%平均准确率（比纯LLM基线高6.36个百分点），同时降低20.4%成本；λ=0.6时达到54.55%准确率，LLM令牌比率仅1.90%，每次示例平均仅0.012次LLM调用，总成本从49.36美元降至1.78美元。

Conclusion: 实验表明，学习到的词元级控制令牌策略能够在不牺牲推理性能的前提下大幅减少LLM使用，证实了细粒度SLM-LLM协作是一种有效且经济的推理范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide strong reasoning capabilities but are expensive to serve at scale, whereas small language models (SLMs) are cheaper but less reliable on difficult problems. We introduce PyroDash, a cost-aware framework for token-level SLM-LLM collaborative inference. During generation, the SLM decides whether to request assistance by emitting a control token. A Collaborate Engine then sends the query and partial reasoning trace to a frozen LLM for completion through a single handoff. The policy is internalized in the SLM, requiring neither a separate router, LLM retraining, nor access to LLM logits. PyroDash trains the SLM in three stages: control-token embedding learning, offloading-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and cost-aware alignment with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Its reward balances answer accuracy against inference cost normalized by LLM-only inference. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, PyroDash supports different accuracy-cost operating points. With $λ=0.05$, it achieves 64.04 percent average accuracy, 6.36 percentage points above the LLM-only baseline, while reducing cost by 20.4 percent. With $λ=0.6$, it achieves 54.55 percent accuracy with a 1.90 percent LLM token ratio and 0.012 LLM calls per example, reducing total cost from USD 49.36 to USD 1.78. These results show that learned token-level handoffs can reduce LLM use while preserving strong reasoning performance.

</details>


### [44] [Generative AI floods and dilutes the market for books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20349)
*Tuhin Chakrabarty,Xinyue Liu,Jane C. Ginsburg,Paramveer Dhillon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于Amazon 2023-2026年14,419本自出版类型小说的全文本AI检测与日销售数据，发现AI生成内容（>25%）的书籍虽占比大但销售额占比小，却持续侵蚀市场：数量增长19.2倍但收入仅增长8.9倍，无AI文本的书籍在高AI渗透类型中损失最大，且畅销AI书籍与现有书籍的语言重叠度更高。生成式AI通过规模而非质量重塑创意市场，这对版权合理使用抗辩有直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验生成式AI能否通过数量规模（而非质量）在商业上取得重要地位，挑战"AI生成书籍是低质量垃圾、买家会忽视"的普遍假设，并为版权合理使用抗辩中的市场影响问题提供实证证据。

Method: 对Amazon上2023-2026年14,419本自出版类型小说进行全文本AI检测（>25% AI文本判定为"大量AI内容"），匹配至2026年6月的日销售记录，分析AI内容书籍的市场渗透率、销售额变化、排名分布及语言独特性（与现有书籍的重叠度）。

Result: 大量AI文本书籍在目录中占比大但销售额占比小；但数量季度增长19.2倍，收入仅增长8.9倍，收入/书下降；无AI文本书籍在高AI扩散类型和Kindle Unlimited中损失最大；畅销AI书籍与现有书籍的语言重叠度更高，且重叠随收入增加而上升（无AI书籍无此梯度）。

Conclusion: 生成式AI可通过规模而非质量重塑创意市场，这对版权合理使用抗辩中的市场影响评估具有直接意义。

Abstract: Generative AI can produce book-length works of fiction at near-zero cost. These books are often dismissed as low-quality ``slop'' that buyers will ignore, and are assumed to carry little commercial weight. We test that assumption with full-text AI detection across 14,419 self-published genre-fiction books sold on Amazon from 2023 to 2026, matched to daily sales records through June 2026. None of these books disclose whether or not they contain AI-produced content. We find that books for which we detected substantial AI text ($>$ 25\%) make up a large share of the catalog but a smaller share of sales. Even so, they reach commercial scale, winning a growing share of sales over time and taking more of the scarce top-rank positions once held by books with no detected AI text. Over this period, the number of books with observed sales in a quarter grew 19.2-fold, while quarterly revenue grew only 8.9-fold. The market therefore added selling books faster than it added revenue, and revenue per selling book fell across most genres. Books with no AI text lose the most ground in genres with high AI diffusion, and most of all where Kindle Unlimited availability is high. Among top-selling books, those with substantial AI text draw on more distinctive language from existing books than do books with no AI text; for these books overlap rises with revenue, a gradient we do not detect for books with no AI text. Generative AI can thus reshape a creative market through scale rather than quality. Our results bear directly on the market-effect question at the center of the fair use defense to copyright infringement.

</details>


### [45] [Notes to Self: Can LLMs Benefit from Experiential Abstractions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20372)
*Chang Liu,Xinyu Li,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否像人类一样从经验中提炼可重用的抽象知识（如策略或警示），并通过这些抽象知识逐步提升问题解决能力。实验表明，从MATH训练集的解题轨迹中提取的自然语言抽象，无论是通过推理时检索还是强化学习增强训练，都能显著提升LLM在数学和逻辑推理基准上的表现。自提取抽象与教师提取抽象效果相当，且该框架可迁移至其他数据集和模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs是否能够像人类一样从经验中蒸馏出可重用的抽象知识（如策略和警示），并利用这些抽象知识来提升解决问题能力，从而弥合人类经验学习与LLM学习之间的差距。

Method: 从LLMs在MATH训练集上的解题轨迹中，由更强的教师模型或LLMs自身提取自然语言抽象，构建可检索的抽象库；然后通过两种方式使用这些抽象：(1)推理时检索，(2)在强化学习中使用抽象增强的训练提示。

Result: 经验性抽象显著提升了LLM在数学和逻辑推理基准上的表现；自提取抽象与教师提取抽象效果相当；该抽象使用框架可有效迁移到其他数据集和模型上。

Conclusion: LLMs能够像人类利用蒸馏经验一样，从自身经验中提取并应用可重用的抽象知识，从而提升推理能力，且该范式具有良好的泛化性和迁移性。

Abstract: Humans distill experience into reusable abstractions, e.g., strategies and cautionary reminders, and apply them to gradually solve problems more effectively. We study whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly benefit from such experiential abstractions. From LLMs' solution traces on the MATH training set, a stronger teacher or the LLMs themselves extract natural-language abstractions into a retrievable library. We explore two usage modes: (1) inference-time retrieval and (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with abstraction-augmented training prompts. Experiential abstractions improve LLM performance on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks. Self-extracted abstractions match teacher-extracted ones, and our abstraction usage framework can transfer to other datasets and models. These findings suggest LLMs can extract and apply experiential abstractions much as humans leverage distilled experience.

</details>


### [46] [LKValues: Aligning Large Language Models with Sri Lankan Societal Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20410)
*Nethmi Muthugala,Supryadi,Surangika Ranathunga,Nisansa de Silva,Ruijie Tao,Ovindu Gunatunga,Pengyun Zhu,Shaowei Zhang,Jingting Zheng,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LKValues 是首个针对斯里兰卡价值观对齐的调查驱动资源套件，包含从205份三语调查中提炼的40项核心价值观、15万条僧伽罗语-英语指令数据集 LKvaluesIT 和1000条评估基准 LKvaluesBench。实验表明大语言模型在低资源文化和价值观对齐上存在差距，LKValues 微调能改善 Qwen 系列模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型价值对齐普遍偏向西方规范，忽视斯里兰卡等多元语言社会的本土文化价值观，且缺乏僧伽罗语语境下的评估数据与微调资源。

Method: 通过205名受访者的三语调查，融合全球框架与LLM提取的本土构念，归纳40项多数认可的社会价值观；据此构建15万条僧伽罗语-英语新闻衍生指令数据集 LKvaluesIT 和1000条评估基准 LKvaluesBench；对三个开源基座模型进行微调并在基准上评估。

Result: 新模型和大模型在低资源语言和文化价值对齐上仍存在显著差距；LKValues 微调提升了 Qwen 系列模型在英语和僧伽罗语上的表现，减少了无效输出和跨语言差异，但改进效果因模型家族而异。

Conclusion: LKValues 能有效嵌入斯里兰卡价值观，提供了一个可复制的低资源、国别特定多元价值对齐流程，数据集已公开发布。

Abstract: Value alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) has been shown to be culturally biased toward Western norms. This results in the mishandling of local values in multilingual societies such as Sri Lanka that have their unique cultural dynamics. Existing benchmarks overlook Sri Lankan-contextualized values in its official language Sinhala, hindering culturally sensitive evaluation and fine-tuning. To bridge this gap, we propose LKValues, the first survey-grounded resource suite for Sri Lankan value alignment. From a trilingual survey of 205 respondents, blending adapted global frameworks and LLM-elicited local constructs, we derive 40 majority-endorsed societal values. Using these values, we construct LKvaluesIT, a Sinhala-English news-derived instruction corpus containing 150k scenario-based instances, and LKvaluesBench, a value-sensitive evaluation benchmark of 1,000 instances. We evaluate a set of proprietary and open-weight LLMs with LKvaluesBench. We fine-tune three open-weight base models (Qwen3.5-4B-Base, Qwen3.5-9B-Base, and Aya-Expanse-8B-Base). Our experiments show that newer and larger LLMs still exhibit low-resource and cultural value-alignment gaps. LKValues fine-tuning improves Qwen-family models in English and Sinhala, reducing invalid outputs and cross-lingual disparities, though gains remain model-family dependent. These highlight LKValues efficacy in embedding Sri Lankan values, offering a replicable pipeline for low-resource, country-specific pluralist value alignment. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/NextME14/LKValues.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

### [47] [BaseRT: Advancing Best-in-Class LLM Inference with Apple M5 Neural Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19438)
*Fabian Waschkowski,Prabod Rathnayaka,Lukas Wesemann*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍BaseRT，一个基于Apple M5芯片Metal 4 API的原生推理运行时，通过利用M5 GPU每个核心内置的专用神经加速器（张量核心），在推理吞吐量上显著超越llama.cpp和MLX。在15种模型配置上，BaseRT的prompt处理吞吐量最高达到llama.cpp的6.4倍、MLX的3.9倍，decode速度最高达llama.cpp的1.75倍、MLX的1.33倍，创下Apple Silicon设备端LLM推理性能新记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple M5芯片引入了重新设计的GPU架构，每个核心配备专用神经加速器（矩阵单元），但现有推理框架（如llama.cpp和MLX）未能充分利用这些硬件能力。作者旨在通过原生Metal 4张量核心API，释放M5神经加速器在LLM推理中的潜力，建立Apple Silicon上设备端推理的新性能天花板。

Method: 基于BaseRT框架（无框架设计），新增一系列手写Metal 4张量核心内核，包括密集和混合专家（MoE）GEMM内核以及flash-attention预填充内核。将计算密集的矩阵乘法路由至M5神经加速器处理，而内存受限的decode路径仍使用原有的特化内核，实现计算与内存瓶颈的分离优化。

Result: 在Apple M5 Pro上，针对15种模型配置（Qwen3、Qwen3.5/3.6、Llama 3.2、Gemma 4系列，参数规模从<1B到35B），BaseRT的prompt处理吞吐量最高达llama.cpp的6.4倍、MLX的3.9倍（在矩阵乘法占主导的MoE模型上提升最大）；decode速度最高达llama.cpp的1.75倍、MLX的1.33倍。

Conclusion: BaseRT证明了M5张量核心是Apple Silicon上prompt处理性能的决定性杠杆，为Apple硬件上的设备端LLM推理设立了新的性能基准。代码已开源。

Abstract: Apple's M5 generation introduces a redesigned GPU architecture in which every core carries a dedicated Neural Accelerator: on-die matrix units exposed through the Metal~4 tensor API. We show that BaseRT, our native Metal inference runtime for large language models on Apple Silicon, exploits these units to push inference throughput on Apple hardware substantially beyond both llama.cpp and MLX. Building on BaseRT's framework-free design, we add a family of hand-written Metal~4 tensor-core kernels (including dense and mixture-of-experts GEMM and flash-attention prefill kernels) that route the compute-bound matrix multiplications of inference through the M5 Neural Accelerators while leaving the memory-bound decode path on our existing specialised kernels. On an Apple M5 Pro, across fifteen model configurations spanning the Qwen3, Qwen3.5/3.6, Llama~3.2, and Gemma~4 families from sub-1B to 35B parameters, BaseRT delivers up to $6.4\times$ higher prompt-processing throughput than llama.cpp and $3.9\times$ higher than MLX, with the largest margins on the mixture-of-experts models where matrix multiplication dominates, while maintaining its lead on decode of up to $1.75\times$ over llama.cpp and $1.33\times$ over MLX. These results establish a new performance ceiling for on-device LLM inference and show that the M5's tensor cores are the decisive lever for prompt processing on Apple Silicon. BaseRT is publicly available at https://github.com/basecompute/baseRT.

</details>


### [48] [Opto-ViT-v2: Noise-Resilient On-Chip Fine-Tuning for Photonic Near-Sensor Vision Transformer Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19421)
*Xuming Chen,Deniz Najafi,Mehrdad Morsali,Chengwei Zhou,Zahra Ghanaatianjobzari,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi,Gourav Datta*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Opto-ViT-v2 是首个在近传感硅光 ViT 加速器上实现参数高效微调的框架，通过张量化低秩分解（仅需 8K 可训练参数）和梯度累积稀疏分类器，在考虑 MRR 串扰、热漂移和激光噪声的实际光噪条件下，实现 0.3-0.8% 精度恢复和 >100 KFPS/W 能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硅光加速器虽支持 ViT 推理，但片上微调面临激活存储大、MRR 权重回写频繁、器件噪声容忍度差等挑战，缺乏有效的 PEFT 框架。

Method: 1) 张量化低秩分解：将预训练光权重与少量可训练电子因子分离（ViT-Base 仅 8K 参数）；2) 梯度累积稀疏分类器：通过一次性 top-k 梯度掩码冻结低重要性权重，减少约 40% 分类器训练成本；3) 首个光子片上训练系统级噪声模型，涵盖 MRR 串扰、热漂移和激光幅度噪声，基于 200+ 器件实测标定。

Result: 在 VTAB-1K（19 任务）和 FGVC 小样本基准上，Opto-ViT-v2 在实测光噪声下恢复精度达干净软件精度的 0.3-0.8%，能效超过 100 KFPS/W。

Conclusion: 低秩因子更新在相同噪声条件下比全微调和传统逐层 LoRA 更鲁棒，实现了光子边缘视觉系统的实际片上域自适应。

Abstract: Silicon-photonic (SiPh) accelerators have emerged as a promising platform for Vision Transformer (ViT) inference by performing matrix multiplications on microring-resonator (MRR) banks with high throughput and energy efficiency. Extending these platforms to support on-chip fine-tuning remains challenging because backpropagation requires large activation storage, frequent weight write-back to MRRs, and tolerance to device-level noise. We present Opto-ViT-v2, the first framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) on a near-sensor SiPh ViT accelerator. Our tensorized low-rank decomposition separates pretrained optical weights from a small set of trainable electronic factors (as few as 8K parameters for ViT-Base), greatly reducing activation storage and weight updates while enabling practical on-chip training. We further introduce a gradient-accumulated sparse classifier that freezes low-importance weights through one-shot top-k gradient masking, reducing classifier training cost by about 40 percent. We also develop the first system-level noise model for photonic on-chip training, capturing the effects of MRR crosstalk, thermal drift, and laser amplitude noise during both forward and backward propagation. Calibrated using measurements from more than 200 fabricated MRR devices, the model shows that low-rank factor updates are more robust than full fine-tuning and conventional layer-wise low-rank adaptation under identical noise conditions. Experiments on VTAB-1K (19 tasks) and FGVC few-shot benchmarks demonstrate that Opto-ViT-v2 recovers within 0.3 to 0.8 percent of clean software accuracy under measured photonic noise while achieving more than 100 KFPS/W, enabling practical on-chip domain adaptation for photonic edge vision systems.

</details>


### [49] [BRIM: Workload-Balanced Dual-Sided Bit-Serial Sparse Inference Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19431)
*Varun Manjunath,Ruokai Yin,Donghyun Lee,Arkapravo Ghosh,Priyadarshini Panda*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BRIM是一种针对双面比特串行稀疏加速器的硬件-软件协同设计方案，通过循环平衡剪枝（CBP）和成对时隙捐赠机制解决工作负载不平衡问题，在CNN、ViT和LLM上实现了超过90%的PE利用率，相比现有双面设计提速2.37倍、能效提升1.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双面比特串行加速器虽能利用两个操作数的比特级稀疏性来减少DNN推理开销，但存在严重的工作负载不平衡问题：每个并发权重-激活对的执行成本取决于两个独立变化操作数的非零比特位数的乘积，导致计算快的PE等待慢的PE，PE利用率仅56-64%，限制了加速效果。

Method: BRIM采用两种集成机制：1) 循环平衡剪枝(CBP)，一种训练后权重优化方法，基于激活统计量重塑权重表示，离线均衡并发处理对的预期工作负载；2) 成对时隙捐赠，一种轻量级硬件机制，以极小的面积开销吸收残差的运行时不平衡。

Result: 在等面积约束下，在CNN、ViT和LLM上评估，BRIM实现了超过90%的PE利用率，相比现有双面设计最高2.37倍加速，最高1.63倍能效提升。

Conclusion: BRIM通过软硬件协同设计有效解决了双面比特串行加速器中的工作负载不平衡瓶颈，显著提升了PE利用率和整体性能，为高效DNN推理提供了新的加速方案。

Abstract: Bit-serial accelerators exploit bit-level sparsity to reduce DNN inference cost, but existing designs exploit sparsity on only one operand, bounding the speedup. Extending sparsity exploitation to both operands simultaneously yields compounding reductions in partial products but introduces a critical new bottleneck: workload imbalance. Because each concurrent weight - activation pair's execution cost depends on the product of two independently varying operand non-zero bit counts, pairs that must complete together finish at vastly different times, leaving faster computations idle. We show this limits PE utilization to 56 - 64% in existing dual-sided designs. We present BRIM, a hardware - software co-designed dual-sided bit-serial sparse accelerator that directly targets this bottleneck. BRIM combines two integrated mechanisms: 1) Cyclic-Balanced Pruning (CBP), a post-training weight optimization that reshapes weight representations based on profiled activation statistics to equalize expected workloads across concurrently processed pairs offline; and 2) Pairwise Slot Donation, a lightweight hardware mechanism that absorbs residual runtime imbalance with negligible area overhead. Evaluated across CNNs, ViTs, and LLMs under iso-area constraints, BRIM achieves over 90% PE utilization, up to 2.37x speedup, and up to 1.63x energy efficiency improvement over prior dual-sided designs.

</details>


### [50] [From Bit-Position Sensitivity to Unequal Error Protection for DNN Inference Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19623)
*Muhammad Husnain Mubarik,Karthik Mohan Kumar,Pedro Antonio Pena,Keshavan Varadarajan,Kunal Tyagi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过分析16种ML工作负载（包括Transformer和CNN）在不同浮点格式下的比特级故障敏感性，发现存在一个安全位阈值Xsafe：低于该阈值的低阶分数位翻转对任务指标影响不足1%，而高阶位和指数位翻转会导致灾难性崩溃。基于此提出非均匀错误保护（UEP）方案，将ECC开销节省37.5-62.5%，面积减少27.8%，读取能耗降低约17%，仅引入约4%的双分区宏面积开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的均匀SECDED保护方案以12.5%的存储开销平等保护每一位，但ML推理中不同比特位对任务准确性的影响差异极大，低阶位无关紧要而高阶位和指数位至关重要，因此需要根据比特位敏感性差异设计更高效的非均匀保护方案。

Method: 对16种ML工作负载（Transformer和CNN）在FP16、BF16、FP32三种浮点格式下进行确定性单比特应力测试，逐位刻画故障敏感性；确定每种数据类型的Xsafe下限；针对不同工作负载类别（扩散模型、视觉编码器、NLU模型、LLM）建立分层保护区间；设计带数据类型标签的UEP编解码器和双分区SRAM架构。

Result: 确定了各格式的Xsafe下限（FP16:6, BF16:4, FP32:15）；在870+次故障注入验证中，选择性保护在连续2位和3位翻转下仍然有效；ECC开销节省37.5-62.5%，ECC面积减少27.8%，BF16读取能耗降低约17%，双分区宏面积开销约4%。

Conclusion: 低阶比特位对ML推理结果影响极小，可利用这一特性设计非均匀错误保护方案，在保证任务指标的前提下显著降低ECC存储开销、面积和能耗。文本条件扩散模型需要最保守的保护，而视觉编码器、NLU模型和鲁棒性强的LLM可容忍更宽的非保护区域。

Abstract: We characterize per-bit-position fault sensitivity in ML inference across 16 workloads -- spanning transformer-based models and attention-free CNNs -- and across three floating-point formats. Our central empirical finding is a sharp bit-sensitivity transition: flipping any of the least-significant fraction bits up to a data-type-specific threshold, Xsafe, degrades task metrics by less than 1% under deterministic single-bit stress tests. Sensitivity rises through the upper fraction bits and spikes at the exponent-mantissa boundary, where a single-bit flip causes catastrophic collapse. Because low-order bits are largely inconsequential while high-order and exponent bits are critical, uniform SECDED protection -- which guards every bit equally at 12.5% storage overhead -- is unnecessarily conservative. We derive per-data-type Xsafe floors (FP16: 6, BF16: 4, FP32: 15) and workload-aware tiers that widen the unprotected region for resilient model classes, raising ECC savings to 37.5-62.5% without retraining. Text-conditioned diffusion models dictate the conservative floor; vision encoders, NLU models, and resilient LLMs tolerate wider bypass regions. These floors and tiers drive an Unequal Error Protection (UEP) codec with per-cacheline data-type tags and a dual-partition SRAM architecture for ML accelerators. Validation across 870+ fault-injection runs confirms selective protection holds under contiguous 2- and 3-bit upsets. The codec reduces ECC area by 27.8% relative to uniform SECDED; dual-voltage operation of the non-critical partition lowers gross BF16 read energy by about 17%, with a roughly 4% dual-partition macro-area overhead.

</details>


### [51] [Leveraging ECRAM for Edge Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19661)
*Nabila Tasnim,Haoran Liu,Qing Cao,Saugata Ghose*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLASP，首个针对持续学习的端到端存内计算（IMC）加速系统，通过软硬件协同设计，利用自研ECRAM器件克服IMC的噪声计算和资源效率低的问题，在MNIST数据集上实现67倍加速和132倍能效提升，精度接近GPU训练水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算平台需要在动态环境中实时适应新数据，持续学习虽能避免灾难性遗忘，但数据在CPU/GPU与内存间的频繁移动导致能耗和延迟过高，限制了其在边缘平台的适用性。IMC可减少数据移动，但受限于计算噪声大和对资源高效训练支持不足。

Method: CLASP采用软硬件协同设计：硬件上使用自研的BEOL兼容ECRAM器件替代传统存算器件，克服噪声问题；软件上提供可被ML算法无约束集成的汇编级指令，支持多种持续学习算法。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上，CLASP使用ECRAM的持续学习（Learning without Forgetting和Experience Replay）达到接近GPU训练的精度，同时实现67倍加速和132倍能效提升。

Conclusion: CLASP证明了存内计算结合ECRAM器件可以使持续学习在边缘平台上高效可行，兼顾精度、速度和能耗，为边缘智能提供了一种有潜力的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Several edge computing platforms, such as autonomous vehicles and smart sensing devices, need to adapt to dynamic environments in real time by learning from new data in the field. Continual learning has emerged as a promising solution for edge training, by incorporating techniques that successfully combine a highly summarized version of previously trained data (to avoid catastrophic forgetting) with recently sensed data. However, as is the case with other ML algorithms, continual learning generates significant data movement between general-purpose CPUs/GPUs and memory, impacting the suitability of continual learning for edge platforms. In-memory computing (IMC; also known as processing-using-memory) can curtail this waste and make continual learning feasible at the edge, but it faces two unique challenges: (1) IMC architectures make use of noisy computation operations that significantly harm training accuracy; and (2) IMC architectures have poor and often incomplete support for resource-efficient training.
  To address these challenges, we propose CLASP (the Continual Learning Acceleration System Platform), which to our knowledge is the first end-to-end system with IMC acceleration for continual learning. The hardware and software of CLASP are co-designed to support a wide range of continual learning algorithms, through software-visible assembly-level instructions that can be incorporated without constraints into ML-based algorithms. CLASP is designed around a back-end-of-line (BEOL) compatible ECRAM device that we fabricate, which can overcome the challenges of IMC-based training using other emerging memory devices. We show that CLASP with ECRAM approaches the accuracy of in-GPU training, while delivering a speedup of 67x and energy savings of 132x for learning without forgetting and experience replay using MNIST.

</details>


### [52] [Revisiting Hardware Priority Queue Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19881)
*Qihang Wu,Austin Rovinski*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在現代FPGA平台上實現並評估了多種代表性硬件優先級隊列架構，填補了該領域缺乏全面比較的空白，所有代碼和測試結果均已開源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件優先級隊列在延遲敏感的應用（如網絡和機器人技術）中能顯著提升性能，但先前研究存在兩個問題：一是早期架構在現代硬件上的相關性存疑；二是缺乏不同架構間的全面比較，難以評估其在性能、資源利用率和可擴展性方面的權衡。

Method: 在現代FPGA平台上實現了多種具有代表性的硬件優先級隊列架構，並進行了定量分析和比較。

Result: 提供了不同硬件優先級隊列架構在性能、資源利用率和可擴展性方面的定量對比結果。

Conclusion: 該研究填補了硬件優先級隊列領域缺乏全面比較的空白，並通過開源庫為未來設計選擇提供了指導。

Abstract: Priority queues - data structures that serve elements based on priority rather than insertion order - are fundamental in a wide range of applications, including operating systems, graph algorithms, and data compression. Software implementations, typically based on binary heaps with O(log N) complexity, are sufficient for many scenarios; however they can become performance bottlenecks in latency-sensitive domains such as networking and robotics. Hardware-based priority queues exploit parallelism to significantly reduce operation latency, delivering critical performance improvements in latency-sensitive applications.
  Despite the breadth of prior work on hardware priority queues, two major challenges remain. First, many foundational architectures were proposed and studied years ago, calling into question their relevance given modern hardware advancements. Second, comprehensive comparisons across different architectures are lacking, making it difficult to evaluate trade-offs in performance, resource utilization, and scalability. This paper addresses both gaps by implementing and evaluating several representative hardware priority queue architectures on modern FPGA platforms and providing a quantitative analysis to guide future design choices. All implementations, tests, and analyses are available through our open-source library at https://github.com/realise-lab/hwpq.

</details>


### [53] [DGNA: Dissecting GPU NUMA Architecture through Microbenchmarking and Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19922)
*Changxi Liu,Yun Chen,Trevor E. Carlson*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出DGNA方法，通过微基准测试和高斯混合模型揭示NVIDIA A100/H100 GPU内存层次结构中的非均匀内存访问（NUMA）架构，首次详细描述了L2缓存和DRAM层面的NUMA节点结构、SM-NUMA关系及缓存一致性维持策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU计算核心飞速发展，但内存效率成为瓶颈。理解GPU内存架构（特别是NUMA机制）对优化应用、设计新架构和构建精确模拟器至关重要，但现有GPU（如NVIDIA/AMD）是黑盒，缺乏对其内存子系统NUMA架构的详细认知。

Method: 提出DGNA方法：通过微基准测试测量L2缓存和DRAM延迟，不依赖架构内部指令；应用高斯混合模型过滤异常值并准确确定延迟分布。在NVIDIA A100和H100上进行了实验。

Result: 成功揭示了A100和H100的NUMA节点架构、SM与NUMA之间的映射关系，以及用于维持缓存一致性的NUMA感知内存分配策略。

Conclusion: 首次详细揭示了GPU内存子系统的NUMA架构，为GPU应用优化、架构设计和模拟器构建提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Graphics Processing Units (GPUs), due to their immense parallel processing capabilities, have become essential across various fields, including gaming and artificial intelligence. With significant advancements in GPU cores, GPU memory efficiency has lagged, resulting in bottlenecks that can limit workload efficiency. To bridge this gap, a deep understanding of GPU memory architectures, particularly Non-Uniform Memory Access (NUMA) mechanisms within L2 and DRAM, is essential for optimizing applications, designing new architectures, and building accurate simulators. However, the latest GPU hardware from vendors like NVIDIA and AMD is still a black-box, making it challenging for researchers to understand the details of their design.
  In this paper, we introduce DGNA, a methodology designed to unveil the NUMA architecture of the GPU memory hierarchy through microbenchmarking and data analysis. Specifically, we propose an approach to measuring the latency of L2 caches and DRAM without relying on the intrinsic instructions of the architecture and apply a Gaussian mixture model to filter out outliers and accurately determine latency distributions. We apply DGNA on NVIDIA's A100 and H100 GPUs, revealing NUMA node architecture, SM-NUMA relationships, and NUMA-aware memory allocation strategies used to maintain cache coherence. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper to detail the NUMA architecture within the GPU memory subsystem.

</details>


### [54] [Formal Foundations for Known Good Reliable Die Screening in Chiplet-Based AI Systems-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20141)
*Prashanthi Metku,Chandra Gandu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了从已知良好芯片（KGD）到已知良好可靠芯片（KGRD）筛选的转变，将其建模为装配前可观测性不完全条件下的约束推理问题，并提出了贝叶斯概率风险模型、安全门控决策架构、不确定性感知的处置边界和约束闭环反馈机制四项贡献，通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证了所有理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGD筛选能保证装配前的功能正确性，但无法提供装配后可靠性寿命的概率性保证，存在半导体测试方法论上的根本性空白。

Method: 将KGD到KGRD筛选形式化为不完全可观测性下的约束推理问题，提出：(i)贝叶斯概率风险模型，映射装配前遥测数据到装配后失效概率并量化观测偏差界；(ii)安全门控决策架构，提供可证明的装配后失效概率保证；(iii)基于贝叶斯最优决策理论的不确定性感知处置边界；(iv)约束闭环反馈机制，在不违反可靠性约束下实现模型持续改进。

Result: 基于N=4,000个合成芯片的蒙特卡洛模拟验证了全部四项理论性质，确认安全保证在所测试的全部门限阈值范围内一致成立。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从KGD到KGRD的理论框架，填补了芯片后组装可靠性概率保证的方法论空白。

Abstract: The rapid growth of chiplet-based artificial intelligence systems-on-chip (SoCs) has exposed a fundamental gap in semiconductor test methodology. Existing Known Good Die (KGD) screening guarantees pre-assembly functional correctness, yet it offers no probabilistic assurance of post-assembly reliability lifetime. To address this limitation, the present work formalizes the transition from KGD to Known Good Reliable Die (KGRD) screening as a constrained inference problem over incomplete pre-assembly observability. Building upon this formulation, four interlocking contributions are presented: (i) a Bayesian probabilistic risk model that maps pre-assembly telemetry to post-assembly failure likelihood with a quantified observability bias bound; (ii) a safety-gated decision architecture that provides a provable post-assembly failure probability guarantee; (iii) uncertainty-aware disposition boundaries derived from Bayes-optimal decision theory; and (iv) a constrained closed-loop feedback mechanism that delivers consistent model improvement without violating reliability constraints. A Monte Carlo simulation study on N = 4,000 synthetic dies verifies all four theoretical properties and confirms that the safety guarantee holds uniformly across the full range of tested gate threshold.

</details>

### [55] [Black-Box Performance Evaluation of Elastic Block Storage: Contract, Rate-Limiting Model, and Software Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20319)
*Yingjia Wang,Ming-Chang Yang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文从用户视角对AWS和阿里云弹性块存储（ESSD）进行黑盒性能表征，提出了包含四个行为观察和五个可操作建议的ESSD契约、一个结合带宽-IOPS双限流与细粒度令牌补充的I/O限速模型，以及针对RocksDB的四个优化指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从云提供商角度记录EBS架构，但缺乏从用户视角对ESSD与本地SSD的性能差异分析，以及主机软件应如何适配的系统性研究。

Method: 采用黑盒性能表征方法，对AWS和阿里云的ESSD进行用户端性能测量与分析；提出I/O限速模型（带宽-IOPS双限流+细粒度令牌补充）；以RocksDB为例进行案例研究。

Result: (1) ESSD契约：4个行为观察+5个可操作建议；(2) 改进的I/O限速模型可抑制延迟尖峰；(3) RocksDB案例得出缓存管理、I/O调节、存储预算利用和压缩算法四个优化指南。

Conclusion: 研究成果可作为EBS用户理解和利用ESSD独特性能特性的实用参考。

Abstract: Elastic block storage (EBS) with the storage-compute disaggregated architecture is a key component in modern cloud infrastructure. EBS offers users storage resources in the form of elastic solid-state drives (ESSDs). Nonetheless, despite recent efforts that have documented EBS architectures from the provider's perspective, how ESSDs perform differently from local SSDs and how host software should adapt accordingly have not been sufficiently studied.
  In this paper, we conduct a user-centric, black-box performance characterization of ESSDs from Amazon AWS and Alibaba Cloud. We make three main contributions: (1) an ESSD contract that presents four behavioral observations and five actionable implications for software adaptation, (2) a refined I/O rate-limiting model combining bandwidth-IOPS dual limiting and fine-grained token refilling to suppress latency spikes, and (3) a case study on RocksDB that derives four guidelines on cache management, I/O regulation, storage budget utilization, and compression algorithms. Collectively, we hope these contributions can serve as a practical reference for EBS users to understand and exploit the distinctive performance properties of ESSDs.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [56] [Fine-grained Computation-Communication Overlap via Tile-level Signaling and Scheduling for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19539)
*Minyu Cui,Anna Wingkvist,Morgan Ericsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度的生产者-消费者协同设计方法，通过tile级信号通知和调度，将MoE模型中的专家计算与第二次all-to-all通信重叠，从而减少通信延迟对关键路径的影响，在4-A100平台上实现了最高2.64倍端到端加速和2.74倍MoE层加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE实现中，专家计算完成后再发起返回all-to-all通信，通信延迟暴露在关键路径上，降低了GPU利用率。现有方法缺乏细粒度的计算-通信重叠机制。

Method: 提出生产者-消费者协同设计：(1) 持久化per-rank计算内核（生产者），覆盖所有本地专家，消除重复内核启动开销，优先处理远程关键tile；(2) 持久化通信内核（消费者），运行于小部分专用SM分区上，在tile就绪后立即发起segment粒度的传输。

Result: 在4-A100平台上对三种MoE模型进行测试，相比四种现有MoE系统，实现了最高2.64倍端到端加速和2.74倍MoE层加速；在不同GEMM形状、路由模式及生产者/消费者SM分区比例下均保持性能提升并保证正确性。

Conclusion: 该细粒度重叠方法无需侵入式修改底层计算算子或通信原语，实用性强，能有效提升多GPU系统上分布式MoE的执行效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity without proportionally increasing computation cost and have become a key building block for scaling large language models (LLMs) to trillion-parameter regimes. Efficient deployment of these MoE models relies on distributed execution across multiple GPUs, where each MoE layer involves two all-to-all communications: dispatching tokens to expert ranks and returning the expert outputs to their source ranks. Conventional MoE implementations launch this return all-to-all after expert compute completes, exposing communication latency on the critical path and reducing GPU utilization. We present a fine-grained approach that overlaps expert compute with the second all-to-all via tile-level signaling and scheduling. Our producer-consumer co-design combines: (1) a persistent per-rank computation kernel (producer) that covers all local experts on the rank to eliminate repeated kernel launch overhead and prioritizes remote-critical tiles, and (2) a persistent communication kernel (consumer) on a small dedicated partition of streaming multiprocessors (SMs) that issues segment-granular transfers as tiles become ready. Our co-design avoids intrusive changes to the underlying computation operators or communication primitives, making it practical for improving distributed MoE execution efficiency on multi-GPU systems. On a 4-A100 GPU platform, evaluated on three MoE models against four state-of-the-art MoE systems, our approach achieves up to 2.64x end-to-end speedup and 2.74x MoE-layer speedup. Compared with a conventional non-overlap baseline, our approach consistently improves both operator- and MoE-layer-level performance across varying GEMM shapes, router modes, and a broad range of producer/consumer SM partitions, while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [57] [Odin: Primitive-Level Synchronization for Distributed Point-Based Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19893)
*Zhenxiang Ma,Zeyu He,Yuanzhen Zhou,Zhenyu Yang,Yuchang Zhang,Miao Tao,Rong Fu,Jidong Zhai,Hengjie Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Odin是一个分布式点基神经渲染训练系统，通过用基元级同步替代全局屏障，以及提前调度器和运行时验证机制，在保持重建质量的同时显著提升训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点基神经渲染（PBNR）在训练大场景时，由于每个视图只读写稀疏的、视图相关的子集，全局任务级或迭代级屏障导致同步成为关键路径瓶颈，而非渲染本身。

Method: 提出Odin系统：1) 用基元级同步替代全局屏障；2) 提前调度器利用稳定局部性和阶段顺序识别低冲突重叠窗口；3) 运行时验证基元发布机制确保后续工作观察到可变状态；4) 提供质量优先路径（保持同步训练可见性）和吞吐优先路径（允许小影响延迟读取）。

Result: 在8个GPU上，针对4个现有PBNR管线及13个非城市场景，吞吐量平均提升1.22倍，隐藏82%的关键路径等待时间，且保持重建质量。在MatrixCity混合并行案例中扩展到64个GPU，吞吐量相比Grendel提升最多1.89倍。

Conclusion: Odin通过基元级同步策略有效解决了PBNR分布式训练中的同步瓶颈问题，在不改变渲染器内核、优化器、训练预算或模型容量的前提下显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Point-based neural rendering (PBNR) represents 3D scenes as explicit, trainable primitives and underpins high-quality reconstruction and emerging embodied AI and world-model pipelines. Unlike layer-structured neural networks, PBNR has primitive-indexed dependencies: each view reads and updates only a sparse, view-dependent subset of mutable scene state. As large scenes require distributed training and optimized renderers reduce per-view computation, global task- or iteration-level barriers increasingly place synchronization, rather than rendering, on the critical path. We present Odin, a distributed PBNR training system that replaces global barriers with primitive-level synchronization. Its ahead-of-time scheduler uses stable locality and phase order to identify low-conflict overlap windows, while the runtime validates primitive publication before later work observes mutable state. Odin provides a quality-first path that preserves synchronized-training visibility and a throughput-first path that uses overlap and gradient evidence to admit only small, low-impact delayed reads; structural changes and high-impact cases remain synchronized. Across four existing PBNR pipelines and 13 non-city scenes on 8 GPUs, Odin improves throughput by 1.22 times on average and hides 82% of critical-path wait while preserving reconstruction quality. In a MatrixCity mixed-parallel case study scaling to 64 GPUs, Odin improves throughput over Grendel by up to 1.89 times without changing renderer kernels, optimizers, training budgets, or model capacity.

</details>


### [58] [Ascend to Science: Exploration of AI Chips for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20120)
*Weicheng Xue,Kai Yang,Yongxiang Liu,Baisong Xu,Dengdong Fan,Xianglin Liu,Pengxiang Xu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以昇腾910 NPU为代表性张量计算平台，系统研究了AI加速器在科学计算负载中的适用性条件。通过五项应用研究（HPL-MxP, LRSVD, SGEMM-cube, PQSim, SMC-X），在精度、执行和内存层次上刻画了瓶颈，并提出了异构执行、混合精度数值格式、精度模拟、层次化内存编排和通信计算重叠等优化映射策略。结果表明，当数值公式、执行部署和数据移动被协同处理时，AI原生NPU能够实现数值鲁棒性、竞争性性能和满意的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器（以低精度张量引擎为核心）的快速崛起给HPC社区提出了一个实际问题：此类硬件在何种条件下能够支持需要数值鲁棒性、不规则内存访问和可扩展性的科学计算负载。

Method: 以昇腾910 NPU为代表性张量中心平台，刻画精度、执行和内存层次瓶颈；针对五项应用（HPL-MxP, LRSVD, SGEMM-cube, PQSim, SMC-X）开发并评估特定负载的映射策略，结合异构执行、混合精度数值公式、精度模拟、层次化内存编排和通信计算重叠。

Result: AI原生NPU在协同处理数值公式、执行部署和数据移动的情况下，能够实现数值鲁棒性、竞争性性能和令人满意的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提供了科学计算负载如何适配张量中心架构的最新实践案例研究，区分了可迁移的优化原则和昇腾特定的实现细节。

Abstract: The rapid rise of AI-oriented accelerators has reshaped compute systems around low-precision tensor engines, raising a practical question for the HPC community: under what conditions can such hardware support scientific workloads that demand numerical robustness, irregular memory access, and scalability? Using the Ascend 910 NPU series as a representative tensor-centric platform, we characterize precision, execution, and memory-hierarchy bottlenecks that hinder the direct deployment of scientific codes. We then develop and evaluate workload-specific mappings across five application studies -- HPL-MxP, LRSVD, SGEMM-cube, PQSim, and SMC-X -- combining heterogeneous execution, mixed-precision numerical formulations, precision emulation, hierarchical memory orchestration, and communication--computation overlap. These studies show that AI-native NPUs can achieve numerical robustness, competitive performance, and satisfactory scalability when numerical formulation, execution placement, and data movement are addressed in a coordinated manner. Our results provide a state-of-the-practice case study of how scientific workloads can be adapted to tensor-centric architectures, while distinguishing transferable optimization principles from Ascend-specific implementation details.

</details>


### [59] [Fully Dynamic Rooted Spanning Tree on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20211)
*Abhijeet Sahu,Harmit Singh,Soham Nandy,G. Ramakrishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种全新的全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入/删除时维护有根生成森林，无需从头重建。在GPU环境下的实验表明，每秒可处理200万次插入和140万次删除，性能显著优于最先进的并行静态算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网络的动态特性要求高效更新生成树结构，而现有并行算法在这一问题上几乎未被探索。

Method: 提出了四种全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入或删除时更新有根生成森林，避免从头重建。

Result: 在GPU环境下，吞吐量达到每秒200万次插入和140万次删除，显著优于最先进的并行静态算法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的并行动态算法在维护生成森林方面具有显著性能优势，填补了该领域并行算法的空白。

Abstract: Spanning trees are fundamental structures in graph theory, essential for various applications such as network maintenance, routing adjustments, and many more. The dynamic nature of real-world networks requires efficient updates to these structures as the underlying graph evolves. Maintaining rooted spanning trees dynamically is particularly crucial for algorithms addressing 2-connected components and minimum-weighted spanning trees. In this paper, we address the challenge of maintaining a rooted spanning forest when a batch of edges are inserted or deleted. We present four novel fully dynamic parallel algorithms to update the spanning forest without reconstructing it from scratch. To the best of our knowledge, parallel algorithms for this problem remain largely unexplored. Our experiments on a diverse collection of real-world graphs using a GPU environment demonstrate a throughput of 2 million insertions and 1.4 million deletions per second, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art parallel static algorithms.

</details>

### [60] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe是一个从全球商业平台收集的多模型LLM服务真实工作负载数据集，揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下的到达动态和令牌行为差异，并提供了用于评估多模型服务平台策略的细粒度工作负载生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务工作负载研究多依赖代理轨迹或粗粒度刻画，未能捕捉现代多模型LLM平台的异构性，缺乏对真实世界中多模型服务动态的深入理解。

Method: 从全球商业市场收集实际多模型LLM服务的工作负载数据FineServe，对到达动态和令牌行为进行细粒度分析，并基于分析结果构建了可组合细粒度模型感知工作负载的生成器。

Result: 揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下存在根本不同的波动模式，并开发了FineServe工作负载生成器，支持为多模型服务平台构建可配置的混合工作负载进行基准测试。

Conclusion: FineServe为评估LLM服务系统中的路由、调度和容量规划策略提供了真实的基础，有助于推动高效LLM服务系统的研究与优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [61] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FraudShield AI 混合框架，整合 LSTM 与图拓扑特征，通过 PageRank 中心性、入度动态和自定义流比率等网络级特征，结合 Focal Loss 和动态阈值机制，在 PaySim 数据集上显著优于逻辑回归和 XGBoost 基线，有效检测极端类别不平衡下的洗钱模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构面临洗钱检测中极端数据不平衡（欺诈率仅 0.13%）和对手规避策略的挑战，传统基于孤立交易的方法难以捕捉 smurfing 和 layering 等复杂洗钱模式。

Method: 提出 FraudShield AI 混合框架：(1) 使用 LSTM 捕获交易时序特征；(2) 手工构建图拓扑特征（PageRank 中心性、入度动态、自定义流比率）捕获网络结构关系；(3) 采用 Focal Loss 处理类别不平衡；(4) 引入动态阈值机制增强对低价值 smurfing 攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 PaySim 数据集上，混合模型在精确率、召回率和 F1 分数上显著优于逻辑回归和 XGBoost 基线，尤其在检测微交易欺诈模式方面表现突出；消融实验验证了时序和拓扑组件的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 从孤立交易分析转向网络级取证可有效提升洗钱检测能力，时序特征与图拓扑特征的融合是应对复杂洗钱模式的关键方向。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [62] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenEvoShield，一种面向LLM多智能体系统的共进化持续防御框架，通过非对称速率控制、动态行为边界、EWC正则化策略集成和多粒度能量检测器，应对攻击策略与正常行为双重漂移，在100轮部署实验中优于静态和持续基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在安全关键场景中面临双重动态威胁：攻击者不断调整注入策略以绕过防御，而正常智能体行为随系统扩展发生漂移。现有防御将部署视为封闭世界问题，一旦分布偏移超出训练覆盖范围便急剧退化。

Method: 提出OpenEvoShield框架，包含四个模块：(M1)非对称速率控制器，从双重漂移信号中解耦攻击侧快速学习率和正常侧慢速学习率；(M2)正常边界更新器，以慢速率维护动态行为边界；(M3)EWC正则化策略集成，实现快速适应而不灾难性遗忘；(M4)基于能量的多粒度检测器，融合节点级、子图级和图级证据将新型攻击分类为分布外样本。

Result: 在5个基准、4种MAS拓扑结构上，跨越100轮部署实验，OpenEvoShield优于静态和持续基线方法，能检测大多数前所未见的新型攻击，同时保持较低的假阳性率。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield通过共进化持续防御范式有效应对了LLM-MAS中攻击策略和正常行为的双重动态漂移问题，为安全关键场景下的多智能体系统提供了更可靠的防御方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [63] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Intel TDX机密实例的单个NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上，对Mistral-7B和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个LLM进行了机密与非机密模式的基准对比。机密模式下，首令牌延迟增加21.8%-27.8%，全局令牌吞吐量下降17.7%-21.1%，闭环吞吐量差距在11.5%-20.2%之间，且大模型更早达到饱和拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算正成为处理敏感输入或保护专有模型资产的AI推理部署的实用需求，但启用GPU加速的LLM服务的机密执行所带来的性能代价尚不明确，需要量化评估以指导容量规划。

Method: 在Intel TDX机密实例的单张NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上，分别以标准非机密模式和机密计算模式运行Mistral-7B v0.1和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个模型，测量首令牌时间、端到端延迟、每请求令牌生成吞吐量、全局令牌吞吐量和闭环请求吞吐量，并在递增并发度下进行固定请求速率和闭环并发实验。

Result: 固定请求速率下，机密模式使平均TTFT增加21.8%（Mistral-7B）和27.8%（Qwen3-30B-A3B），全局令牌吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发实验中，吞吐量差距在11.5%-20.2%之间，但大模型在机密模式下更早达到饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理在负载下仍可保持可用的吞吐量，但容量规划需同时考虑稳定的吞吐量损失以及大模型在机密模式下更早出现的饱和行为。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [64] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FORMULASPIN，一种自我对弈框架，通过利用公式的可执行性作为隐式监督信号，打破监督微调的性能上限，在电子表格公式生成任务上取得SOTA结果（74.9%精确匹配、87.1%执行准确率），无需额外标注数据即可实现迭代式自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电子表格公式生成方法依赖静态监督数据，性能很快饱和。传统自我对弈方法SPIN因对执行等价的不同公式变体产生矛盾梯度而失效。需要一种能区分语义错误与有效风格变体的自改进框架。

Method: FORMULASPIN将训练建模为双人博弈：主玩家学习偏好真实公式而非旧版本生成的结果；执行反馈将输出按粒度排序（语义正确性→风格优化），形成自适应课程学习。同时引入ExecVote投票机制处理多有效公式情形。

Result: 在NL2FORMULA基准上取得74.9%精确匹配和87.1%执行准确率，匹配需要额外偏好标注的模型，超越传统SFT和前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: FORMULASPIN证明了自我对弈在数据稀缺场景下的潜力，并为将该范式扩展到非可执行领域打开了方向。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [65] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息辨别力概念，通过Learn2Discern框架和三个规范性公理评估LLM对信息来源和真实性的辨别能力。实验发现13个模型在源辨别和真相辨别上接近随机水平，过度依赖来源流行度而非可靠性，且模型复杂性和规模无法解决源辨别的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地使用互联网等外部知识源，但它们在整合信息时是否能恰当地区分可靠与不可靠的来源（源辨别），以及是否能判断新信息是否使自身更接近真相（真相辨别），这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 形式化定义信息辨别力；提出Learn2Discern框架，基于三个规范性公理设计可解释指标；通过预注册的用户研究（n=299）验证公理的外部有效性；在13个模型和近67万次试验上进行系统评估。

Result: 模型在源辨别和真相辨别上表现接近随机；模型对来源流行度的依赖程度是可靠性的两倍；无论新信息是否改善其相对于真相的立场，模型更新的程度大致相同；模型在自身先验已较准确的数据集上整合外部知识最有效；更新更大的模型改善了真相辨别，但未改善源辨别；简单推理时干预可改善两种辨别能力。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在信息辨别力上存在系统性缺陷，源辨别盲点无法通过模型扩展解决，需要专门的对齐方法。随着LLM取代传统搜索，这一对齐属性的重要性日益增加。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [66] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS是一个用于LLM智能体运行时安全监控的结构化计划安全监控器，结合确定性规则、参数级检查和校准逻辑回归风险评分，实现对四种干预动作（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订）的分级选择，在多个基准测试上优于纯规则方法，且延迟极低（0.205ms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用工具的LLM智能体越来越多地执行高风险操作，现有的安全监控方法缺乏细粒度的结构化干预策略，亟需一种能够在运行时进行分级安全监控的机制。

Method: NEXUS结合了确定性安全规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分，对四种动作（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修订）实施形式化干预策略，实现分级升级的干预路由。

Result: 在128实例合成基准上F1=0.949、4类干预准确率0.6406（优于纯规则27.3个百分点）；R-Judge基准F1=0.861（vs. 纯规则0.849）；IPI上0% ASR（99%控制允许）；NEXUS-Stress基准F1=0.881；中位延迟0.205ms，开销低于0.1%。

Conclusion: NEXUS作为一种结构化计划安全监控器，在多种基准测试中显著优于纯规则方法，延迟开销极低，适合实际部署，但面对规则盲区（NEXUS-Stress）仍具挑战性。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation. On a 128-instance synthetic benchmark, NEXUS achieves an F1 score of 0.949 and a 4-class intervention accuracy of 0.6406, outperforming rule-only intervention selection by 27.3 percentage points. It also improves over rule-only on R-Judge (F1 = 0.861 vs. 0.849), matches rule-only on AgentHarm due to threat-model limits, and achieves 0% ASR at 99% control allow on IPI. On the rule-blind NEXUS-Stress benchmark, NEXUS reaches an F1 score of 0.881, highlighting the difficulty of fine-grained intervention routing. With 0.205 ms median latency, NEXUS adds under 0.1% overhead to typical agent loops. Code, benchmarks, and the calibrated risk scorer are publicly released.

</details>


### [67] [Stochastic Primal-Dual Decoding for Multiobjective Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19357)
*Dmitrii Moor,Ben Carterette,Senthilkumar Krishnamoorthy,Kyle Kretschman,Denis Beslic,Melissa Yalla,Alice Y Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级推理时解码层，通过在线约束优化（随机原始-对偶近似）增强自回归生成式推荐系统，在不重新训练模型的情况下实现多目标序列生成，并提供了理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统中的多目标方法要么依赖后处理（不适用于生成式序列设定），要么将辅助目标直接融入模型训练（大规模系统中不切实际），缺乏轻量且适配生成式序列特性的多目标方案。

Method: 将解码过程形式化为在线约束优化问题，在逐个生成item时，根据剩余约束松弛度动态调整相关性与辅助目标之间的权衡，通过随机原始-对偶近似方案实现。

Result: 离线实验和在线A/B实验均显示多目标权衡持续提升，辅助目标收益+1.8%，且用户满意度不受影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的推理时解码层无需修改或重新训练基础模型，即可有效实现生成式推荐系统的多目标序列生成，兼顾理论保证与实际性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in recommender systems (RS) have shown substantial performance gains through generative modelling. In practice, recommendation often involves constructing slates -- ordered lists of items -- that must satisfy multiple objectives beyond relevance, such as constraints defined over item attributes or fairness constraints. Existing multiobjective approaches either rely on post-processing techniques designed for non-generative settings, or incorporate auxiliary objectives directly into model training. The former does not explicitly account for the sequential nature of generative RS, while the latter is often impractical in large-scale systems.
  We propose a lightweight, inference-time decoding layer that augments autoregressive generative RS to support multiobjective slate generation without modifying or retraining the underlying model. We formulate decoding as an online constrained optimisation problem, where items are selected sequentially, and trade-offs between relevance and auxiliary objectives are adjusted dynamically based on the remaining constraint slack, i.e., how much of each objective remains to be satisfied. This is implemented via a stochastic primal-dual approximation scheme that balances relevance and auxiliary objectives during generation.
  We provide theoretical guarantees on constraint violation and regret, and evaluate the proposed approach through extensive offline experiments and a large-scale online A/B experiment in a real-world recommender system. Our results show consistent improvements in multiobjective trade-offs, including a +1.8\% gain in the auxiliary objectives achieved at zero cost to user satisfaction.

</details>


### [68] [LISA: Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention for Efficient Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19358)
*Yu Zhao,Zekun Zhang,Fan Jiang,Bo Zeng,Linlong Xu,Shimin Shan,Yu Liu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA（线性索引稀疏注意力），一种即插即用的注意力替代模块，通过并行集成线性注意力（O(n)复杂度）和闪电索引器（选择Top-M重要token进行稀疏自注意力），将推理复杂度从O(n²)降至O(nM)，在16K上下文下实现50%推理加速，并在推理基准上平均提升5.6%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1）在测试时扩展范式下推理上下文长度不断增加，但标准自注意力的O(n²)计算复杂度导致长序列推理成本急剧上升，限制了长CoT推理在生产环境中的部署。

Method: LISA并行集成两个轻量组件：①线性注意力模块提供O(n)时间复杂度的长程记忆；②闪电索引器从全上下文选择Top-M重要token，送入稀疏自注意力。两分支通过门控机制融合。两阶段训练：第一阶段通过知识蒸馏用线性注意力+滑动窗口注意力近似教师模型的完整自注意力分布；第二阶段引入索引器替代静态滑动窗口，使用逐头KL散度损失对齐教师模型注意力模式进行动态token选择。

Result: 在DeepSeek蒸馏Qwen模型上，LISA在16K token上下文下实现50%推理加速，在AIME和MATH-500等推理基准上平均性能提升5.6%。

Conclusion: LISA通过线性注意力和稀疏索引的并行组合，在不需从头预训练的条件下有效降低了长序列推理的计算复杂度，同时提升了推理性能，为长CoT推理模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in long chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 have led to increasingly longer inference context lengths under the test-time scaling paradigm. However, the O(n^2) computational complexity of standard self-attention causes inference costs to grow sharply with long sequences, limiting the deployment of long-CoT reasoning in production settings. To address this, we propose LISA (Linear-Indexed Sparse Attention), a plug-and-play attention replacement module that requires no pretraining from scratch. LISA integrates two lightweight components in parallel within the original model: (1) a Linear Attention module that provides long-range memory with O(n) time complexity; (2) a Lightning Indexer that selects the top-M important tokens from the full context to feed into a Sparse Self-Attention. The two branches are fused via a gating mechanism, reducing inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(nM) (M << n) for generating n tokens. We design a two-stage training pipeline: Stage 1 initializes the model by integrating the linear attention to capture long-range dependencies, complemented by a sliding-window attention mechanism that is optimized via knowledge distillation to approximate the full self-attention distribution of a frozen teacher model. In Stage 2, we further introduce the Indexer to replace the static sliding-window mechanism, enabling dynamic token selection from broader contexts. The Indexer is trained using a novel per-head KL divergence loss, which aligns its selection behavior with the attention patterns of the teacher model. Experiments on DeepSeek-distilled-Qwen models demonstrate that LISA achieves a 50% inference speedup under 16K-token context, while improving average performance by 5.6% on reasoning benchmarks including AIME and MATH-500.

</details>


### [69] [Profile-Graph Memory for LLM Agents: Implicit Cross-Entity Traversal through Narrative Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19359)
*Shengtong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多跳记忆基准 MemHop（1000个问题，跳数1-5，覆盖10个社交网络场景）和双层级记忆架构 ProGraph（包含画像扩展与压缩残差），在 MemHop 上达80.1%，在 LoCoMo 上达78.4%，均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆基准仅评估单跳回忆，缺乏对多跳关联能力的测量。

Method: ProGraph 包含两层：(i) 画像扩展——基于子串匹配的实体名称遍历，作为知识图谱的轻量替代；(ii) 压缩残差——在每次画像更新时零额外API代价共提取精确日期、数量、命名项。

Result: MemHop 上达80.1%（匹配 FullContext），LoCoMo 上达78.4%（超 FullContext 11.3pp）；消融实验显示画像扩展驱动多跳推理（移除后-22.6pp），压缩残差驱动精确召回（未共提取时-8.6pp）。

Conclusion: ProGraph 在两项基准上均超越 Mem0、A-Mem、HippoRAG 和 RAG，验证了所提方法在长期记忆与多跳推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that interact across sessions, yet current memory benchmarks primarily evaluate single-hop recall, leaving multi-hop association largely unmeasured. We make three contributions. First, we introduce MemHop, a multi-hop memory benchmark of 1,000 questions at hop depths 1-5 across 10 social-network scenarios, with per-hop evidence annotations. Second, we present Profile-Graph Memory (ProGraph), a two-layer memory architecture combining (i) profile expansion -- substring-matched traversal of entity names that naturally appear in LLM-written profile narratives, a minimal alternative to explicit knowledge-graph construction -- and (ii) compression residuals -- exact dates, quantities, and named items co-extracted with each profile update at zero extra API cost. Third, a full-grid ablation shows cross-benchmark mechanism specialization: profile expansion drives multi-hop reasoning (-22.6pp on MemHop when removed) while compression residuals drive precision recall (-8.6pp on LoCoMo when not co-extracted), with cross-effects under 3pp within a single architecture.
