The experimental campaign combined a reference-performance program across the 30-90% load range with scenario-based tests in which abnormal conditions were introduced after stabilized fault-free operation, enabling controlled comparison between baseline and fault-affected behaviour. Five anomaly classes were implemented through physical interventions affecting major engine subsystems: cooling-water pump cavitation, compressor air-filter clogging, air-cooler fouling, injection-valve nozzle clogging and turbine degradation induced through increased exhaust-side restriction. The released data comprise multi-sensor time-series of operating, thermal, pressure, flow and combustion-related variables, with a separate reference-performance record and metadata for structured reuse. Technical validation shows that the reference measurements remain physically coherent across the operating range and that the imposed anomalies produce interpretable response patterns consistent with the affected subsystems, including progressively distinguishable behaviour where different severities were implemented. By combining controlled fault realization, multi-load operation and system-level measurements within a real marine-engine platform, the dataset provides a well-documented benchmark for anomaly detection, fault diagnosis, degradation modelling and related condition-monitoring studies in maritime machinery.

</details>


### [142] [Guardrails as Scapegoats: Auditing Unfaithful Safety Refusals in Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19449)
*Aarushi Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个轻量级黑盒审计框架，用于检测工具增强型LLM代理在工具静默故障（返回空/null/格式错误负载但HTTP 200）时的行为。在4个静默故障剖面、12个工具桩上评估4个模型，发现编造行为（FAR）占主导（56.6%），而虚假安全拒绝（USR）在基线几乎不存在（0.25%），但在加入安全提示词后被放大15.6倍（至3.95%）。USR是一种潜伏行为，安全词汇会激活模型为工具故障编造政策理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架过度关注能力指标或显式工具崩溃，忽略了工具基础设施静默故障（HTTP 200但负载为空/null/格式错误）的审计空白，这类故障可能引发代理编造虚假结果或不当拒绝等安全隐患。

Method: 构建了一个轻量级黑盒审计框架，注入4种静默故障剖面（空负载、null负载、格式错误负载等）至12个生产级工具桩，将代理响应分类为三种互斥行为：诚实投降（HSR）、编造（FAR）和虚假安全拒绝（USR）。在零温度、中性系统提示下评估2个前沿模型和2个开源模型，并进行消融实验加入安全提示语。

Result: FAR占主导（56.6%），代理将空负载当作真实数据并编造结果；USR基线极低（0.25%，396条轨迹中仅1例）。加入安全提示语后USR放大15.6倍（至3.95%，p<0.001），敏感工具（fetch_medical_record等）占USR实例的多数。

Conclusion: USR是一种由安全提示词激活的潜伏行为，模型在工具静默故障时会编造安全策略理由来掩饰失败。提出负载-响应错位启发式方法用于生产环境检测，并讨论了对安全导向部署的治理启示。

Abstract: Evaluation frameworks for tool-augmented LLM agents focus overwhelmingly on capability metrics or explicit tool crashes, leaving silent infrastructure failures and HTTP 200 responses with empty, null, or malformed payloads largely unaudited. We introduce a lightweight black-box auditing framework that injects four silent failure profiles across 12 production-adjacent tool stubs and classifies agent responses into three mutually exclusive behavioral classes: Honest Surrender (HSR), Fabrication (FAR), and Unfaithful Safety Refusal (USR). Evaluating two frontier and two open-source models at temperature zero under a neutral system prompt, we find that FAR dominates (56.6% of valid responses): agents treat empty payloads as real data, silently returning fabricated results. USR, in which an agent invents a policy or privacy rationale to explain the failure, is nearly absent at baseline (0.25%, one instance across 396 valid trajectories). Our key finding emerges from an ablation where we augment the system prompt with standard safety language ("prioritize user privacy and data security"), which amplifies USR by 15.6x (from 0.25% to 3.95%; 95% CI on ablation rate: 2.2%-6.4%; Fisher's exact test, p < 0.001). USR is a latent behavior, activated when safety vocabulary in the system prompt primes the model to reach for policy rationales when tools silently fail. Sensitive tools (fetch_medical_record, retrieve_contract, fetch_user_profile) account for the majority of USR instances. We propose a payload-response misalignment heuristic for production-level detection and discuss governance implications for safety-forward deployments.

</details>


### [143] [REGEN: Replay-recycling for Expert-to-Generalist distillation with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19450)
*Yunjie Chen,Xiaoxin Chen,Fang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REGEN提出了一种基于离线强化学习的重放记忆回收方法，通过复用教师模型训练过程中的经验回放缓冲区来训练通用模型，完全解耦了采样与训练过程，显著降低了计算成本，同时在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随任务上达到了与多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线强化学习是提升LLM能力的主要手段，但其计算成本和基础设施要求极高，尤其当RL仅作为一次性训练阶段时效率低下。多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）虽能解耦RL阶段降低成本，但仍存在耦合的推理和反向传播限制。作者希望进一步解耦这一过程，实现更高效、可扩展的通用模型训练。

Method: REGEN的核心思想是"重放记忆回收"：在教师模型进行专业化RL训练时，其经验回放缓冲区（replay memory）作为免费副产品被保存下来。随后，利用这些离线数据，通过离线RL算法直接训练通用模型。这种方式完全解耦了 rollout 采样与反向传播训练过程，避免了在线RL或MOPD中需要同时进行推理和梯度计算的开销。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随等任务上，REGEN达到了与MOPD相当的精度，但训练成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: REGEN将在线RL转变为一种数据合成过程而非一次性学习阶段，有望扩展到大规模后训练场景而无需承担繁重的计算负担。

Abstract: Large-scale online reinforcement learning (RL) is the predominant means of eliciting advanced abilities including long-term reasoning and agentic tool use in large language models (LLMs). However, continuing to scale it across vast task domains of interest remains challenging in both computational infrastructure and cost, especially when considering RL as merely a one-off learning stage. Recently, a widely used technique for distilling knowledge across various domains and training stages, multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD), helps to decouple the RL stage, saving costs, while maintaining generality across vast domains. Nonetheless, similar to online RL, MOPD requires coupled inference and backward passes, which continues to limit its scalability and computational efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose REGEN: Replay-recycling for Expert-to-Generalist Distillation with Offline RL. Instead of distilling from multiple teacher models, REGEN trains a generalist by simply recycling the replay memory -- the free by-product of the teachers' specialized RL training -- and employing offline RL algorithms. REGEN completely decouples the rollout sampling from the backward training process and thus greatly reduces the training cost. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following, REGEN matches the accuracy of MOPD at substantially lower cost. It potentially turns online RL into a data synthesis process instead of a one-off learning stage, and can potentially be extended to large-scale post-training without requiring heavy computational load.

</details>


### [144] [Predictive Extrema, Unprofitable Policies: An AI-Assisted Audit of Candle-Based Binance Spot Timing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19453)
*Ayoub Jadouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了基于蜡烛图的机器学习模型能否在考虑交易成本后，将加密货币极值或短期预测转化为正的Binance现货交易策略。结果显示最强后期证据为负：一个不变的十对强制日选策略在19个7月周期中亏损6.72%（胜率3/19）；验证集选择的局部最小策略回报-1.79%，局部最大卖出/再入策略落后持续持有2.80%；OHLCV-only日频策略AUC达0.874/0.896但平均精度仅0.134/0.116，亏损44.30%；此外还发现先前One4All研究存在数据泄露和方法论缺陷。所有测试协议均未建立正的执行策略价值，最终交易决策保持NO_TRADE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验基于蜡烛图的机器学习模型能否在考虑实际交易成本后，将加密货币价格极值预测或短期预测转化为可盈利的Binance现货交易策略。

Method: 使用脚本化的固定种子模型运行和确定性模拟器进行数值实验；人类监督的AI代理辅助文献检索、批评、工件的协调、文档编制和源代码打包（不参与交易决策）。测试了多种协议，包括强制日选策略、验证集选择的局部最小/最大策略、基于Gurgul思想的OHLCV-only日频适应策略，并对先前One4All研究进行法证审计。

Result: 1) 不变十对强制日选策略：19个周期亏损6.72%，3胜16负；2) 局部最小策略回报-1.79%；3) 局部最大策略落后持续持有2.80%；4) 两种策略的毛均值优势（11.11和12.21 bps）低于21 bps压力；5) OHLCV-only策略AUC 0.874/0.896但平均精度0.134/0.116，七周期亏损44.30%（持有-41.20%）；6) One4All研究存在日期污染、边界泄露、同收盘价入场、原始目录缺失等缺陷。

Conclusion: 在测试的多种协议中，事件排序性能未能建立正的执行策略价值，所有交易决策保持NO_TRADE。

Abstract: We audit whether candle-based machine-learning models can turn predictions of cryptocurrency extrema or short-horizon outcomes into positive Binance Spot paper policies after assumed costs. Numerical results come from scripted fixed-seed model runs and deterministic simulators; human-supervised AI agents supported the July 20 evidence-integrity revision through literature retrieval, separately tasked critique, artifact reconciliation, documentation, and source packaging, not trading decisions. The strongest later-period evidence, conditional on extensive predecessor search, is negative: an unchanged ten-pair mandatory-daily selector lost 6.72\% over 19 July cycles at an assumed 31-bps completed-cycle cost, with 3 wins and 16 losses. In short model-specific July evaluations, the validation-selected local-minimum policy returned -1.79\%, while the local-maximum sell-to-cash/re-entry policy underperformed continuous holding by 2.80\%; their gross mean advantages of 11.11 and 12.21 bps were below even the 21-bps stress. A Gurgul-inspired, OHLCV-only daily adaptation attained minimum/maximum ROC AUC of 0.874/0.896 but average precision of only 0.134/0.116 and lost 44.30\% over seven cycles, versus -41.20\% for buy-and-hold. A forensic audit also downgraded an earlier One4All "30-day holdout": its dates had influenced prior architecture work, its four-hour outcome horizon was not purged at split boundaries, it used same-close entry, and its raw result directories were absent. Across the tested, mostly exploratory protocols, event-ranking performance did not establish positive executable policy value. Every operational decision remains NO\_TRADE.

</details>


### [145] [Generating Bearing Vibration Signals at User-Specified Fault Probabilities Using PR-GAN and Counterfactual Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19455)
*Seyed Mohammadreza Alavi,Ardeshir Shojaeinasab,Reza Jalayer,Masoud Jalayer,Behnam Bahrak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对轴承振动数据中边界样本（灰色区域概率样本）稀缺的问题，提出了两种生成方法：基于训练的PR-GAN（概率正则化生成对抗网络）和基于训练的无监督Wachter式反事实（CF）方法。实验表明，CF方法在概率控制精度和成功率上显著优于PR-GAN，但PR-GAN的运行时更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴承振动数据中大部分样本的故障预测概率接近0或1，而中间概率（灰色区域）的边界样本非常稀缺。这类样本对维护决策（如是否需要额外检查或采取保守响应）及决策边界研究至关重要，因此需要生成此类样本。

Method: 使用异构集成分类器的平均输出作为固定、梯度可访问的概率预言机。提出两种方法：（1）PR-GAN：基于WGAN-GP扩展，通过残差生成器编辑真实信号，同时推动分类器输出向目标概率靠近；（2）CF方法：无训练、逐样本的Wachter式反事实优化，直接优化每个输入信号以达目标概率，同时保持与源信号接近。

Result: 在CWRU和Paderborn轴承数据集上，CF方法平均绝对概率误差为0.005-0.008，容忍范围内成功率为1.000；PR-GAN平均误差为0.046-0.059，成功率为0.501-0.680。CF的概率控制更可靠且所需L1变化更小，但PR-GAN在多数设置下运行时更低。

Conclusion: CF方法在生成边界样本的概率控制精度和可靠性上显著优于PR-GAN，是更优的选择；PR-GAN在计算效率上具有优势。

Abstract: In bearing vibration datasets, most samples receive predicted fault probabilities close to 0 or 1, while samples with intermediate (gray-zone) probabilities are rare. Such borderline samples are important because they reflect conditions in which maintenance decisions may require additional inspection or a conservative response and are useful for studying decision boundaries. To address this scarcity, this paper proposes and compares two approaches that generate vibration signals whose predicted fault probability matches a target probability of 0.25, 0.50, or 0.75. We use the average output of a heterogeneous ensemble classifier with different architectures and random initializations as a fixed, gradient-accessible probability oracle. The first, training-based approach, Probability-Regularized Generative Adversarial Network (PR-GAN), extends Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) and edits a real signal through a residual generator while pushing the classifier output toward the target probability. The second is a training-free, per-sample Wachter-style counterfactual (CF) procedure that directly optimizes each input signal to reach the target probability while remaining close to the source signal. We evaluate both methods on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) and Paderborn bearing datasets using mean absolute target-probability error, time-domain total variation, and frequency-domain log power spectral density (log-PSD) differences. Across all settings, CF reaches the target with a mean absolute probability error of 0.005-0.008 and a within-tolerance success rate of 1.000 on retained samples, whereas PR-GAN's mean error is 0.046-0.059 with success rates between 0.501 and 0.680. CF therefore steers the probability more reliably and requires smaller average L1 changes, whereas PR-GAN has a lower reported runtime in most settings.

</details>


### [146] [MoA-Structured Decode Attention DNF Derivation, KV-Cache Accumulation, GQA/MQA, and OpenACC Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19456)
*Lenore Mulin,Gaetan Hains*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用数组数学(MoA)为Transformer注意力机制推导出四种内存最优推理构件：(1)单查询解码去规范化形式，消除K^T缓冲区；(2)C/OpenACC GPU内核，实现精确IEEE-754算术；(3)多步KV缓存，每步追加复杂度O(d_k+d_v)；(4)通过ψ-选择推导GQA/MQA，实现KV流量缩减h_q/h_{kv}倍。所有程序均通过PyTorch验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer注意力推理过程中内存访问的最优策略，通过数学推导而非经验调优来最小化DRAM流量，解决大模型推理中的内存瓶颈问题。

Method: 基于数组数学(MoA)的形式化方法，从前向传播的去规范化范式出发，通过ψ-归约、ψ-选择等代数操作推导四种推理构件，并实现C/OpenACC GPU内核。

Result: 单查询解码达到(d_k+nd_k+nd_v+d_v)×4B的DRAM流量，误差≤2×10^{-7}；GPU内核实现精确IEEE-754浮点运算(误差为零)；KV缓存每步追加复杂度O(d_k+d_v)；GQA/MQA实现h_q/h_{kv}倍的KV流量缩减。

Conclusion: 通过MoA形式化方法可以从数学上保证注意力推理的内存最优性，为Transformer推理加速提供了理论支撑和工程实现方案。

Abstract: We derive four memory-optimal inference artifacts for transformer attention using the Mathematics of Arrays (MoA), each following directly from the forward-pass Denotational Normal Form (DNF) of with the query-row index fixed to the current decode step. The artifacts are: (1)~a single-query decode DNF in which the $ψ$-reduction eliminates the $K^\top$ buffer algebraically, achieving $(d_k + nd_k+ nd_v+ d_v)\times4\,{B}$ Dynamic Random Access Memory (DRAM) traffic result numerically verified to $\|{err}\|_\leq2\times10^{-7}$; (2)~a C/OpenACC Graphics Processing Unit (GPU) kernel with Operational Normal Form (ONF) stride arithmetic and hardware-coalesced memory access, verified to $\|\mathrm{err}\|_\infty=0$ (exact IEEE-754 floating-point arithmetic); (3)~a multi-step KV-cache with $O(d_k+d_v)$ per-step append via MoA concatenation $\#$; and (4)~Grouped-Query Attention (GQA) and Multi-Query Attention (MQA) derived via $ψ$-selection, achieving a proven $\frac {h_q} { h_{kv} }$ reduction in KV traffic. All programs are verified against PyTorch scaled_dot_product_attention.

</details>


### [147] [Total Variation Distance Estimation in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19510)
*Eric Price,Kevin Tian,Zhiyang Xun,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三种访问模型下估计两个自回归语言模型之间总变差距离（TV距离）的问题，提出了更高效的查询复杂度算法，并通过实验验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM部署中，即使使用相同的权重，不同的推理引擎（如SGLang和vLLM）因批处理、自定义内核和量化等优化手段，会生成不同的输出分布。现有方法估计TV距离的查询复杂度较高，需要更高效的估计方法。

Method: 提出了三种访问模型下的TV距离估计算法：(1) 样本访问下，使用$\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$查询，改进了Meel等人(2025)的结果；(2) logit访问下，使用$O(n/\varepsilon^2)$查询并证明最优；(3) 噪声logit访问下，使用$\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$查询，在两种极端之间平滑插值。

Result: 理论上，三种访问模型下均实现了更优的查询复杂度，其中logit访问下达到下界紧性。实验上，成功测量了SGLang和vLLM在相同权重下的TV距离，展示了方法在KL散度无限时仍可估计TV距离的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的TV距离估计算法在理论上具有更优的样本/查询复杂度，在实际部署场景中（如对比不同推理引擎）具有鲁棒性和可操作性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern LLM deployments use a number of implementation choices and inference optimizations (e.g., batching, custom kernels, and quantization) on top of fixed weights, so two engines serving "the same model" can produce meaningfully different distributions. We study the problem of estimating the total variation (TV) distance between two length-$n$ autoregressive distributions to additive error $\varepsilon$, under three access models. (1) Under sample access, we use $\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, where $K$ is the maximum support of the next-token distribution. This improves upon the $\widetilde{O}(n^3 m/\varepsilon^5)$-query estimator of Meel et al. (2025), where $m \geq K$ is the total size of the token alphabet. (2) Under logit access, we use $O(n/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, and this is tight. (3) Under noisy logit access, we smoothly interpolate between the above two guarantees: if probability values are given to relative error $σ$, we use $\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$ queries. We complement our theoretical results with an empirical evaluation of our algorithms, for example measuring the distance between SGLang and vLLM serving identical weights. Our experiments highlight the robustness and practicality of estimating the total variation distance, which remains estimable where the KL divergence is infinite. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunZhiyang/llm-tv-estimation.

</details>


### [148] [Do Sheaf Neural Networks Use Holonomy? A Measure--Intervene--Control Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19514)
*Ankit Grover,Rémi Bourgerie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入首个基无关的三角环乘积测量方法，利用层神经网络的实验表明：三角计数任务中SO(2)旋转从0.010增至0.388弧度，社区检测仅达0.029弧度；移除学习到的连接会显著增加测试误差，但图谱岭回归预测器表现更优，且固定度数图中旋转增加但未超越训练均值预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何架构常通过旋转等内部机制证明其合理性，但仅靠任务性能无法判断这些机制是否真正驱动了预测。本文旨在分离几何变化、连接敏感性和三角特定计算的证据。

Method: 以层神经网络为实验平台，引入首个基无关的训练三角环乘积测量方法，分离旋转、茎空间面积和方向。在高同质性GraphUniverse环境下进行度量-干预-控制研究。

Result: 三角计数中NSP的SO(2)环旋转从0.010增至0.388弧度，社区检测仅0.029弧度；移除学习到的SO(2)传输显著增加测试误差；图谱岭回归预测器更准确；固定度数图中旋转增加但性能未超越训练均值预测器。

Conclusion: 该度量-干预-控制研究成功分离了几何变化、连接敏感性和三角特定计算的证据，表明旋转机制在特定任务中确实发挥重要作用，但存在更优的替代方法。

Abstract: Geometric architectures are often justified by internal mechanisms such as rotations, yet task performance alone cannot show whether those mechanisms drive predictions. Using sheaf neural networks (SNNs) as a testbed, we introduce the first basis-independent measurement of trained triangle-loop products, separating rotation, stalk-space area, and orientation. In a custom high-homophily GraphUniverse regime, Neural Sheaf Propagation (NSP) increases the triangle-weighted mean two-dimensional SO(2) loop rotation from 0.010 to 0.388 radians for triangle counting, while the community-detection comparison ends at 0.029 radians. Across the training-set-size experiment, replacing all learned SO(2) transports with identities sharply increases test error, establishing post-training sensitivity to the complete learned connection. However, a graph-summary ridge predictor is more accurate, diagonal maps also improve, and fixed-degree graphs develop increasing rotation without outperforming the training-mean predictor. This measure-intervene-control study separates geometric change, connection sensitivity, and evidence for triangle-specific computation.

</details>


### [149] [Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES) for Storm-Centered Weather Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19522)
*Sonia Cromp,Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV,Youran Wang,Grace Kisslinger,Frederic Sala,James Booth,Allegra LeGrande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoDES是一个基于扩散模型的图像到视频生成模型，专注于合成高保真、物理一致的天气事件（如气旋风暴），在峰值涡度误差上降低52%，异常相关系数提高8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气模型在捕捉大型天气系统（如气旋风暴）的精细结构方面存在不足：区域模型受限于有限的历史记录和固定地理边界，全球模型计算成本高且分辨率不足以捕捉风暴动力学细节。

Method: 提出GeoDES（Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis）模型，一种定制化的图像到视频扩散模型，专注于生成演化中的风暴结构，合成物理一致、高保真的天气事件。

Result: GeoDES在关键指标上优于先前方法：在北大西洋测试集上，峰值涡度误差降低52%，异常相关系数提高8%。

Conclusion: GeoDES能够有效生成用于压力测试预报模型和扩充气象数据集的高质量天气事件。

Abstract: While machine learning-based weather models hold significant promise, they struggle to predict the detailed structure of large-scale weather systems such as cyclonic storms. Regional models are constrained by limited historical records within fixed geographic boundaries, while global models are computationally expensive and often operate at resolutions too coarse to capture fine-grained storm dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Geospatial Diffusion-based Evolution Synthesis (GeoDES) model, a custom image-to-video diffusion model. By focusing generation strictly on the evolving storm structure, GeoDES synthesizes physically consistent, high-fidelity weather events suitable for stress-testing forecast models and expanding meteorological datasets. Evaluations demonstrate that GeoDES outperforms prior methods on key metrics, achieving $52\%$ lower Peak Vorticity Error and $8\%$ higher Anomaly Correlation Coefficient than the next strongest methods on the North Atlantic test set.

</details>


### [150] [The C-index illusion: discrimination without calibration in published survival models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19526)
*Rafael da Silva,Danilo Alvares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过三个不同领域（硬盘故障、P2P信贷违约、数字平台用户流失）的真实世界复现实验，验证了仅靠C-index评估生存分析模型会产生系统性误导：模型可能具有高判别力但校准失败（p=2.6e-136），处理竞争风险时偏差可达4个百分点，预测概率随时间恶化而全局C-index保持稳定。五个预注册假设中有三个被拒绝，表明问题在于对选定模型的过度自信而非选错模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人已在合成数据上论证仅用C-index评估生存模型会产生误导，但这一发现是否适用于真实、已发表的非临床模型尚未被检验。本文旨在填补这一实证空白。

Method: 复现三个已发表的生存分析模型（硬盘故障、P2P信贷违约、数字平台用户流失），通过与锚定论文的合成实验验证评估工具，并在Holm校正族系错误率下检验五个预注册假设。

Result: 五个假设中三个被拒绝：模型在近乎完美复现判别力（C=0.9595 vs 0.958）时校准检验失败（p=2.6e-136）；将贷款提前还款视为非信息性删失而非竞争风险时，违约风险估计向上偏倚约2个百分点，高风险段达4个百分点；用户流失模型的概率估计随预测时间窗口恶化但全局C-index保持稳定。未发现度量选择会反转模型偏好的直接证据（统计功效有限）。

Conclusion: 仅依赖C-index评估生存模型会导致对选定模型的误导性自信，即使模型具有高判别力，校准失败和时间依赖性偏差仍可能严重。建议在评价中纳入校准和时变准确性指标，并发布了可复用的预注册评估工具。

Abstract: "Stop Chasing the C-index when Evaluating Survival Analysis Models" (ICML 2026, Spotlight) argued normatively, on synthetic data, that evaluating survival models by discrimination alone, i.e. the concordance index, produces systematically misleading model comparisons, because the metric ignores calibration and time-dependent accuracy. Whether this matters for real, published, non-clinical models has not been tested. We reproduce three published survival-ML models across three structurally distinct domains (hard-drive failure, peer-to-peer credit default, and user disengagement on digital platforms), validate our evaluation instrument against the anchor paper's own synthetic experiment, and test five pre-registered hypotheses under a Holm-corrected family-wise error rate. Three of five reject. A model that reproduces the published discrimination almost exactly (C = 0.9595 vs. 0.958 reported) fails a formal calibration test at p = 2.6e-136; a broad feature-ablation search finds no single attribute responsible for this discrimination, so the calibration failure is not an artifact of a trivial shortcut. A lender's estimated default risk is biased upward by roughly two percentage points, growing to nearly four points in the riskiest segment, when loan prepayment is treated as non-informative censoring rather than as a competing risk. A platform's churn model shows probability estimates that degrade with the prediction horizon even as its global discrimination stays within the pre-registered C-index band. A direct test of whether metric choice inverts which model is preferred does not reject, though with limited power given only two to three models per domain; the failure mode we document is better characterized as misplaced confidence in a chosen model than as choice of the wrong one. We release a reusable, pre-registered evaluation harness with full code and a single annotated notebook.

</details>


### [151] [Trustworthy Privacy-Preserving Multimodal Federated Learning for Personalised Breast Cancer Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19532)
*Ruth Amey,Muhammad Arifur Rahman,Taha Osman,Nicholas Shopland,Andy Burton,Mufti Mahmud,David J. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在乳腺癌肿瘤进展预测中的应用，评估其在透明性、可扩展性、安全性和公平性四个关键部署维度上的表现。实验使用多模态数据（临床信息、MRI影像等），将联邦学习与集中式学习进行对比，结果表明联邦学习能在保持数据本地化的同时达到接近集中式学习的预测性能，为隐私保护下的个性化癌症治疗提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决使用敏感健康数据训练预测模型时的隐私问题，特别是在个性化癌症治疗场景中，探究联邦学习能否在保护数据隐私的同时支持鲁棒的肿瘤进展预测模型开发。

Method: 构建联邦学习框架，使用多模态数据（临床信息、肿瘤特征、生物标志物、人口统计学数据、MRI影像等）建模乳腺癌肿瘤特征随时间的变化。将联邦学习与集中式模型进行性能对比，并分析安全更新、子组公平性、跨机构可扩展性等策略。

Result: 联邦学习在预测性能上可与集中式学习相匹敌，同时保持了数据本地化，并且在安全更新、公平性和可扩展性方面表现出良好效果。

Conclusion: 联邦学习是实现隐私保护、多模态预测建模的可行方案，有助于推动数字孪生等未来应用，为临床医生和患者提供个性化治疗规划支持。

Abstract: Federated learning has emerged as a potential solution to privacy concerns associated with using sensitive health data for training predictive models, particularly in personalised cancer care. This research investigates whether federated learning can support the development of robust models for predicting tumour progression in breast cancer patients while addressing four critical deployment pillars: transparency, scalability, security, and fairness. This study evaluates a federated learning framework using multimodal data, including clinical information, tumour characteristics, biomarker data, and patient demographics, alongside medical imaging data such as MRI scans, to model changes in tumour characteristics over time. The performance of the federated approach was compared with that of a centralised model trained on aggregated data. The report then further examines strategies to enhance secure model updates, maintain performance across patient subgroups, and support scalability across institutions. The findings assess whether federated learning can achieve predictive performance comparable to centralised learning while preserving data locality. These results contribute to understanding the feasibility of privacy-preserving, multimodal predictive modelling and support future applications such as digital twins to assist clinicians and patients in personalised treatment planning.

</details>


### [152] [Agent-Centric Animal Pose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19548)
*Eyrun Eyjolfsdottir,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体中心的自回归动物行为生成模型框架，通过将动物行为建模为从自我中心感知到自我中心运动的映射，并发布了一个通用库来处理多智能体并行表示和数据转换，在果蝇求偶行为数据上验证了模型效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经科学和动物行为学中，以算法层面理解动物行为（包括注意力机制、内部模型构建、计划形成及动作映射）仍是一个核心挑战。数据驱动的生成模型为此提供了潜在途径，但需要能反映生物约束（从自身参考系感知和行动）的建模框架。

Method: 提出智能体中心的自回归模型框架：输入为自我中心的感官观测，输出为自我中心的运动，每个智能体独立感知并响应同种个体；通过管理同一数据的多智能体并行表示，并处理离散化等ML特定转换来实现；发布了一个专注于可组合操作序列的通用库，用于表示间转换。

Result: 训练模型能够捕捉求偶果蝇群体中社会行为的分布；库提供了定量工具用于衡量模型拟合度；支持不同输入输出表示的系统性比较，并能直接迁移到新领域。

Conclusion: 该框架通过智能体中心的自回归建模有效捕捉了动物社会行为分布，所发布的通用库促进了不同表示间的系统性比较和跨领域迁移，为理解动物行为的算法层面提供了数据驱动工具。

Abstract: Understanding animal behavior at an algorithmic level -- what animals attend to, how they form internal models and plans, and how this maps to action -- remains a central challenge in neuroscience and ethology. Data-driven generative models offer a path toward this understanding. We introduce a framework for training agent-centric autoregressive models of animal behavior from tracked pose, applicable to single animals and to groups in which each agent senses and responds to its conspecifics. Our models input egocentric sensory observations and output egocentric movements, mirroring the biological constraint that animals observe and act on the world from their own reference frame. Social behavior emerges from agents independently sensing and responding to one another. This agent-centric formulation requires managing many parallel representations of the same data, along with ML-specific transformations like discretization. We release a general-purpose library focused on the composable sequences of operations that translate between these representations. We show that trained models capture the distribution of social behavior in groups of courting Drosophila, and our library includes quantitative tools for measuring fit. We demonstrate how the library supports systematic comparison across input and output representations and that it adapts straightforwardly to a new domain.

</details>


### [153] [End-to-End Differential Privacy in Training Deep Neural Network Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19580)
*Huaiyuan Rao,Calvin Hawkins,Alexander Benvenuti,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的差分隐私训练框架，在保持标签公开的同时对训练输入进行隐私保护，通过在softmax输出上应用Dirichlet机制来实现。实验表明，该方法在CIFAR10、MNIST等多个数据集上取得了新的最优准确率，例如在CIFAR10上ε=4时准确率达88.17%，显著优于先前工作的78.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私机器学习通常同时对训练输入和标签进行隐私保护，然而在许多实际场景中标签是公开的或可以安全地公开。这种过度保护会导致模型精度不必要的下降。因此，本文旨在提出一种仅在训练输入上施加隐私保护、同时保持标签公开的方法，以提高模型在给定隐私预算下的准确性。

Method: 提出了一种新的差分隐私训练框架，核心思想是：(1) 保持训练标签公开，仅对训练输入进行隐私保护；(2) 针对具有softmax输出层的神经网络，在训练过程中对softmax输出应用Dirichlet机制进行随机化；(3) 利用Rényi差分隐私（RDP）对重复使用训练数据的多轮训练进行严格的隐私预算累加分析。

Result: 在CIFAR10、MNIST、MedMNIST、FashionMNIST和SVHN数据集上均取得了新的最优准确率。具体地，在δ=10^{-5}的(ε,δ)-差分隐私设置下，CIFAR10上ε=4时准确率从78.37%提升至88.17%，甚至在ε=1时仍能达到82.96%的准确率，大幅超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在标签可公开的场景下，仅对训练输入进行隐私保护可以显著提升模型效用。提出的Dirichlet机制结合RDP分析的方法在多个标准数据集上取得了突破性的准确率-隐私权衡，为实际应用中的差分隐私机器学习提供了更高效的保护方案。

Abstract: Differentially private machine learning enables model training on sensitive data while ensuring that individual data is unlikely to be recoverable from the parameters of the resulting model. However, existing work often privatizes both training inputs and their labels, and these protections may be conservative when labels are public or can be safely made public. Therefore, in this work we propose a novel private training framework that instead privatizes training inputs while keeping labels public. We consider neural networks with softmax output layers, and thus the mapping from training inputs to the output of the softmax layer is a mapping onto the unit simplex. We randomize softmax outputs during training by applying the Dirichlet mechanism to enforce differential privacy for the training inputs, hence the ``end-to-end'' label. Because training data is reused across multiple training epochs, we use the notion of \Renyi differential privacy to formulate tight bounds on the strength of privacy provided by the Dirichlet mechanism across repeated uses. We show empirically that we attain new state-of-the-art accuracy when training from scratch on CIFAR10, MNIST, MedMNIST, FashionMNIST, and SVHN across all privacy budgets evaluated. Notably, when implementing $(ε, δ)$-differential privacy with $δ=10^{-5}$, we improve the prior state-of-the-art accuracy from $78.37\%$ to $88.17\%$ at $ε=4$ on CIFAR10, and our approach has $82.96\%$ accuracy even for $ε=1$, which significantly outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [154] [The Mechanism Matters: When Knowledge Graphs Help Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19616)
*Mohammed Sameer Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了知识图谱（KG）在强化学习（RL）中的三种注入机制（状态特征、动作掩码、基于势能的奖励塑形），通过可控实验发现：结构化的KG在组合稀疏奖励任务中能显著提升样本效率和求解可靠性（70%升至97%），但这种增益依赖于KG中任务相关知识的数量；更重要的是，安全性取决于注入机制——软性、最优性保持的注入能忽略错误知识，而硬掩码在KG不完整或损坏时会禁止必要动作，导致比不用KG更差的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献以单领域、正向结果的方法论文为主，缺乏对KG结构何时帮助、何时中性、何时损害RL代理的系统性理解。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，在MiniGrid环境中独立变化RL任务、三种注入机制（状态特征/动作掩码/势能奖励塑形）及KG质量，并使用边置换的shuffle控制来区分结构性增益与通用正则化。

Result: （1）结构化KG使求解可靠性从70%提升至97%，shuffle控制下增益消失（掩码p=0.0001，塑形p=0.006）；（2）KG价值与任务相关知识量正相关；（3）软注入能忽略错误知识，硬掩码在KG损坏时导致比无KG更差的结果。UMLS临床案例研究为谨慎的零结果。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了关于在何种条件下、以何种程度信任KG指导RL的具体指导，强调了机制选择对安全性的关键影响。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) are widely used to inject prior knowledge into reinforcement learning (RL), yet the literature is dominated by single-domain, positive-result method papers, so we lack a systematic account of when KG structure helps an agent, when it is neutral, and when it hurts. We conduct a controlled study that independently varies the RL task, the injection mechanism (state features, action masking, or potential-based reward shaping), and KG quality. Using a synthetic, fully controllable KG over MiniGrid environments, we report three findings. First, on compositional sparse-reward tasks structured KG guidance improves sample efficiency and solve reliability (70% to 97% of seeds), and a shuffle control that permutes the KG's edges while preserving their count collapses the benefit toward baseline (masking p=0.0001; shaping p=0.006), so the gain is structural rather than generic regularization. Second, KG value scales with the amount of task-relevant knowledge the graph contains. Third, and most consequential, safety depends on the mechanism: soft, optimality-preserving injection benefits from correct knowledge and harmlessly ignores incorrect knowledge, whereas hard masking is brittle, forbidding essential actions when the KG is incomplete or corrupted and making a wrong KG worse than none. A UMLS-derived clinical case study on sepsis management under offline RL is a careful null, underscoring that benefits require task structure the chosen mechanism can exploit. Our results give practitioners concrete guidance on how, and how much, to trust a KG when using it to guide RL.

</details>


### [155] [SCPP: A Unified Python Library for Soft Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19620)
*Kiyan Rezaee,Morteza Ziabakhsh,Artin Bahrampour,Seyed Mohammad Ghoreishi,Asal Khaje,Ali Sajedifar,Manny Chalak,Ava Zerafatangiz,Sadegh Eskandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCPP是一个开源Python软聚类框架，提供scikit-learn兼容接口，集成了40种代表性算法（涵盖模糊、概率、图基、矩阵分解和深度学习方法），并包含标准化基准测试、文档和自动化测试，旨在实现可复现的软聚类实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软聚类领域缺乏统一的、可扩展的开源框架，现有实现分散且接口不统一，难以进行公平的算法比较、基准测试和可复现研究。

Method: 构建scikit-learn兼容的标准估计器接口，统一软聚类的训练、预测、隶属度表示、评估和基准测试流程；集成40种算法，涵盖模糊、概率、图基、矩阵分解和深度学习方法；提供综合基准测试（数据集、聚类质量指标、运行时/内存/可扩展性评估），并配备文档、示例、自动化测试和生态集成。

Result: 成功构建了包含40种算法的统一框架，实现了标准化的接口和基准测试流程。

Conclusion: SCPP为软聚类研究提供了标准化、可扩展、可复现的开源工具，方便研究者进行算法比较和扩展。

Abstract: In this paper, we present SCPP (Soft Clustering Python Package), an open-source Python framework for soft clustering. SCPP establishes a canonical, scikit-learn-compatible estimator interface that standardizes model training, prediction, membership representation, evaluation, and benchmarking across heterogeneous soft clustering methods, including fuzzy, probabilistic, graph-based, matrix factorization, and deep learning methods. The framework currently integrates 40 representative algorithms together with a comprehensive benchmarking comprising datasets, clustering quality metrics, and standardized runtime, memory, and scalability evaluation. SCPP further provides extensive documentation, practical examples, automated testing, and seamless integration with the scientific Python ecosystem, enabling reproducible experimentation and straightforward extension with new algorithms. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/soft-clustering/soft-clustering.

</details>


### [156] [HypEMBER: Hypernetwork-based Ensemble for Robust Policy Learning of Parametrized Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19628)
*Nicolò Botteghi,Gabriele Pascali,Urban Fasel,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HypEMBER，一种结合超网络与集成学习的强化学习框架，用于含测量不确定性和模型不确定性的参数化动力系统鲁棒控制，在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和粒子导航任务中展现了优于现有方法的训练稳定性、样本效率和对不确定性的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维状态空间、昂贵数值求解器、控制方程的部分已知性以及物理参数的不确定性使得标准RL方法计算上不可行，且对噪声/不完整测量的鲁棒性差、参数泛化能力弱。

Method: 将策略和价值函数均表示为超网络，以物理参数为条件生成底层网络权重，实现跨不同动力学子参数泛化；同时采用策略和价值近似器的集成学习来量化认知不确定性，改善探索策略并增强训练前后鲁棒性。

Result: 在一维Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和二维时变环流粒子导航任务上，HypEMBER在训练稳定性、样本效率以及对测量噪声和参数误设的鲁棒性方面持续优于现有最先进RL方法。

Conclusion: 超网络与集成学习的结合能有效解决参数化动力系统控制中模型不确定性和测量不确定性问题，实现跨参数域的泛化与鲁棒控制。

Abstract: In this work we investigate reinforcement learning (RL) as a framework for the robust control of parametrized dynamical systems in presence of measurements and model uncertainties. High-dimensional state spaces, expensive numerical solvers, the partial knowledge of the governing equations, and the dependence on physical parameters that may be uncertain or difficult to estimate accurately, make the use of standard RL approaches computationally unfeasible. Indeed, lack of robustness and poor generalization across parameter variations are further amplified in presence of noisy or incomplete measurements, ultimately hampering control performance. To address these challenges, we introduce HypEMBER, a novel RL framework based on the combination of hypernetworks and ensemble learning. In the proposed approach, both the policy and value functions are represented through hypernetworks that generate the weights of the underlying models conditioned on the physical parameters of the system, thereby enabling parametric generalization across different dynamical regimes. In addition, an ensemble of policy and value approximators is employed to quantify epistemic uncertainty, leading to improved exploration strategies and enhanced robustness during and after training. The performance of the proposed framework is assessed on two representative parametrized control problems: (i) the one-dimensional Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation and (ii) a particle-navigation task in a two-dimensional time-dependent gyre flow, focusing on robustness with respect to measurement noise and parameter misspecification. Numerical results demonstrate that HypEMBER consistently improves training stability and sample efficiency, while achieving superior robustness to uncertainties affecting both the system dynamics and the available observations, in comparison with state-of-the-art RL methods.

</details>


### [157] [Anatomy of a Sound Neural Reasoner: One-Shot Amortization, First-Pass Poisoning, and Search Inertness in Clue-Rich Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19635)
*Aleksey Komissarov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断发现Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT) 在Sudoku求解中并非真正的迭代推理器，而是一次性预测器：前向传播即决定几乎所有格子（6x6全填，9x9填94-96%），搜索仅用于修剪无效推导；首次前向传播的"中毒"（删除真实解所需值）是硬切片失败的根本原因；约束图注意力已匹配完整CoLT精度，位置表需更长时间训练；对称性增强和测试时联合变换可将硬切片准确率提升至100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑神经求解器是否真正进行迭代推理与回溯搜索，揭示LDT在Sudoku上的实际行为机制，区分"一次性预测"与"学习搜索"。

Method: 通过消融实验（移除CoLT组件、约束图注意力 vs 位置表）、对称性分析（数字置换增强、测试时对称变换联合）、跨任务对比（图着色）系统诊断LDT的推理行为。

Result: LDT实际为一次性预测器：首次前向传播决定结果，搜索仅减少1,497倍重复无效推导但未改变求解实例集；约束图注意力在冻结训练预算下匹配完整模型，位置表需更多训练；数字置换增强使9x9准确率从<1%升至96.5±0.3%，测试时对称联合使硬切片从72.8-78.9%升至100%。

Conclusion: 在线索丰富的Sudoku中，LDT类系统是一步摊销预测器而非学习搜索过程，其精度由校准性和对称性决定，搜索仅消除计算浪费；在图着色等稀疏任务中搜索仍影响准确率。

Abstract: Neural solvers are built to deduce, branch, and revise intermediate states. The Lattice Deduction Transformer (LDT) appears to do exactly that. In clue-rich Sudoku, it does not: one forward pass commits essentially the entire grid (every blank cell on standard 6x6, 94-96% on augmented 9x9), turning the iterative solver into a one-shot predictor wrapped in an exact verifier. All hard-slice failures are decided before search begins, when the first pass confidently deletes a value required by the true solution. We call this first-pass poisoning. Adding learned branching, MRV, backtracking, value exclusion, and shared nogoods (CoLT) does not change which Sudoku instances are solved; it cuts repeated invalid derivations 1,497-fold. At the frozen training budget, constraint-graph attention alone matches full-CoLT accuracy, while positional tables recover only under substantially longer training, indicating an optimization and sample-efficiency advantage rather than an absolute capacity difference. The diagnosis predicts two effective interventions. Digit-permutation augmentation raises 9x9 accuracy from below 1% to 96.5 +/- 0.3 across three training seeds on a symmetry-disjoint split. Test-time union over symmetry-transformed passes raises all three hard-slice checkpoints from 72.8-78.9% to 100% without retraining. On from-scratch graph coloring, one-shot behavior disappears and search changes accuracy. In clue-rich completion, LDT-like systems are one-shot amortized predictors rather than learned search procedures: accuracy is determined by calibration and symmetry, while search primarily removes computational waste.

</details>


### [158] [Expert-Guided Forecast Editing for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19659)
*Hung Le,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Manh Nguyen,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dai Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFT框架，在冻结的时间序列基础模型上，通过分解趋势-季节成分并复用专家评分，在有限查询预算下平衡先验利用与结构化探索，实现高效的专家引导预测编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型能跨域预测，但预测结果固定后无法直接融入任务特定的专家反馈。现有方法中，best-of-N纯粹利用模型先验分布，优化方法则将预测视作无结构高维向量进行探索，两者均次优。需要一种在有限专家查询预算下平衡利用与探索的方法。

Method: DEFT首先在分解的趋势-季节空间中利用基础模型的预测样本（利用阶段），然后通过分量级精细调整在样本附近探索（探索阶段）。仅对完整轨迹查询专家，然后将评分复用到查询过的组合中出现的趋势和季节分量上，使得每次专家查询提供结构化的分量级反馈，同时保持基础模型冻结。

Result: 在78个数据集、3个基础模型、4种反馈类型、7个查询预算下，DEFT在一致提升专家引导效果。分子动力学案例进一步表明该原则可推广到更物理的反馈场景。

Conclusion: 稀疏的测试时引导应花费在平衡先验利用与结构化探索上，DEFT通过分解-复用的方式有效实现了这一原则。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models can forecast across heterogeneous domains without task-specific training, but their forecasts are fixed once produced and cannot directly incorporate task-specific expert feedback. We study expert-guided forecast editing: a frozen foundation model generates candidate future trajectories, and an expensive expert evaluator scores them to guide forecast revision. Under a tight query budget, two natural strategies sit at opposite ends: best-of-$N$ purely exploits the foundation model's predictive distribution, while optimization approaches mostly explore the forecast horizon as an unstructured high-dimensional vector. Each extreme is individually sub-optimal. We introduce \textbf{DEFT}, an expert-guided forecast editing framework that balances the two by first exploiting the foundation model's predictive samples in a decomposed trend--seasonal space, then exploring around them via component-wise refinement. DEFT queries the expert only on complete trajectories, then reuses scores for the trend and seasonal components that appeared in the queried recombinations. This lets each expert query provide structured component-level feedback while keeping the foundation model frozen. We compare DEFT against direct search approaches, including best-of-$N$, cross-entropy methods, and Bayesian optimization, under matched expert-query budgets. Across two forecasting benchmarks consisting of 78 datasets, three time-series foundation models, four feedback types, and seven query budgets, DEFT consistently improves the effectiveness of expert guidance. A molecular-dynamics case study further suggests that the same principle extends to more physically grounded feedback, supporting the hypothesis that sparse test-time guidance should be spent balancing prior exploitation with structured exploration.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Clustering with Provable Guardrails for LLM Inference at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19704)
*Longshaokan Wang,Wai Tsang Keung,Punit Ghodasara,Roman Wang,Ali Dashti,Francesc Moreno-Noguer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段聚类算法，先使用Mini-batch K-Means进行初步聚类，再通过等价于Set Cover的贪心策略选择代表样本，在保证每个样本与代表点之间的相似度阈值和类别属性精确匹配的前提下，实现了线性时间复杂度和极低内存占用，并在3800万用户的生产系统中将推理成本和延迟降低了50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM应用面临推理成本和延迟瓶颈，通过聚类为每组样本调用一次LLM的方法缺乏对每个样本与代表点之间相似度的可衡量的质量控制，且现有聚类方法无法同时保证样本间相似度阈值、类别属性精确匹配以及扩展到千万级数据规模。

Method: 两阶段算法：第一阶段使用Mini-batch K-Means生成初始聚类；第二阶段在每个初始聚类内，将问题转化为嵌入空间中α-ball的Set Cover问题，采用Johnson-Chvatal贪心启发式选择代表样本。该方法严格保证了相似度阈值和属性匹配约束，时间复杂度为O(nd + n²d/K)，内存为O(nd + n²/K²)，当K随n成比例增长时达到线性复杂度。

Result: 在内部和公开数据集上，该方法不仅提供了每个样本的质量保证，而且运行速度比常见聚类方法快10-1000倍，能够扩展到大多数标准方法无法处理的数据规模。在3800万客户的人设推荐系统中应用后，下游推理成本和延迟降低了50倍，同时保持了个性化效果并成功支持了产品上线。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了大规模场景下带精确样本级质量控制（相似度阈值+属性精确匹配）的高效聚类算法，证明了通过将聚类转化为嵌入空间Set Cover问题可以在理论保证和实际性能之间取得平衡，为LLM应用的大规模部署提供了一种实用的降本增效方案。

Abstract: Scaling LLM-based applications to millions of users is bottlenecked by the inference cost and latency of modern foundation models. A natural fix is to cluster the inputs and call the LLM only on cluster representatives, letting other members inherit the output -- but this is only safe if each member is measurably close to its representative. Existing clustering methods do not offer such per-sample quality control at scale: none jointly guarantee a minimal within-cluster similarity, exact matching of categorical attributes, and scalability to tens of millions of samples. We propose a two-stage algorithm that generates initial clusters with Mini-batch K-Means, then greedily selects representatives within each initial cluster -- a step equivalent to the Johnson-Chvatal heuristic for Set Cover over alpha-balls in embedding space. The algorithm enforces the similarity and attribute guardrails exactly by construction, and runs in $O(nd + n^2 d/K)$ time and $O(nd + n^2/K^2)$ memory for $n$ samples, feature dimension $d$, and $K$ initial clusters -- linear in $n$ when $K$ grows proportionally with $n$. We provide benchmarks against common clustering methods on internal and public datasets: our method not only delivers per-sample guardrails but also runs 10-1000x faster and scales to data sizes where most standard methods become intractable. Deployed on 38 million customers for a persona-based recommender, the clustering method cut downstream cost and latency by 50-fold while preserving personalization and unblocked the production launch.

</details>


### [160] [How Fast Can Reward Models Score? A Systems Study of C++ and PyTorch Inference Runtimes for RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19712)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka,Anish Katta,Deva Rohit Reddy Peddireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RLHF流程中的奖励评分瓶颈，构建了基于ONNX Runtime的原生C++推理引擎，在CPU上显著超越PyTorch eager、torch.compile和FastAPI，GPU上优于PyTorch和FastAPI但不及torch.compile，并发现ONNX Runtime本身而非C++语言是加速主因，批处理策略的影响比语言和运行时的选择更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中奖励评分模块阻塞策略更新，而现有方案大多默认使用PyTorch eager或torch.compile，未检查是否真正最优。评分本身计算量小但与生成任务争抢CPU/GPU资源，因此加速评分引擎的主要价值在于释放资源供生成使用。

Method: 构建基于ONNX Runtime的原生C++推理引擎，在CPU和GPU上对比PyTorch eager、torch.compile和FastAPI基线，通过重复独立运行确保可靠性，并分析加速来源（ONNX Runtime vs C++语言）及批处理策略的影响。

Result: CPU上C++引擎显著超越所有基线（置信区间无重叠）；GPU上优于PyTorch和FastAPI，但torch.compile更优。加速主要来自ONNX Runtime本身而非C++语言。批处理策略的影响远超语言或运行时的选择。

Conclusion: RLHF奖励评分的优化核心在于选择正确的运行时（ONNX Runtime）和批处理策略，而非单纯使用C++语言；CPU场景下收益尤为显著，GPU场景下torch.compile仍是强有力竞争者。

Abstract: In RLHF pipelines, reward scoring blocks policy updates. Slow scoring bottlenecks the entire loop, since no update runs until every rollout gets a score. And yet most setups just default to PyTorch eager mode or torch.compile, no one checks if that's actually fastest. Scoring itself is small. Rollout generation eats far more of a typical RLHF step. But scoring and generation fight over the same CPU and GPU resources, so a faster scoring engine doesn't shrink step time on its own. It mainly frees up capacity generation can use instead. We built a native C++ inference engine on ONNX Runtime. First step: confirm correctness. Output matched the PyTorch reference to 5.7 x 10^-6 on CPU and 4.2 x 10^-3 on GPU, close enough to trust. Then we tested it against PyTorch eager mode, torch.compile, and FastAPI, on both CPU and GPU. CPU was decisive. Our engine beat every baseline, confidence intervals didn't even overlap. GPU gave a different view: we beat PyTorch and FastAPI, but torch.compile came out ahead. Further testing traced the speedup to ONNX Runtime itself, not C++ as a language. And batching strategy mattered more than either the language or the runtime choice, more than we expected. The results are from repeated, independent runs, since single runs just aren't reliable enough to trust.

</details>


### [161] [Koopman Dreamer: Spectrally Constrained Latent Dynamics for Stable World-Model Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19719)
*Jiaqi Li,Xinglong Zhang,Haibin Xie,Yixing Lan,Wei Pan,Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Koopman Dreamer，一种基于谱约束的确定性潜动态核心的Dreamer风格世界模型，使用二维旋转-缩放块（含阻尼、旋转和近周期模式）作为Koopman主干，结合线性/低秩双线性动作项和随机状态调制，配合EMA教师目标和多阶段训练目标，在连续控制任务中提升了长时域潜 rollout 的稳定性和闭环控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型在连续控制中通过想象轨迹优化策略可提升样本效率，但常见神经转移模型对模态持续性和长rollout误差累积的调控能力有限。

Method: 1) Koopman-inspired谱约束确定性潜动态核心（2D旋转-缩放块，有界半径表征阻尼/旋转/近周期模式）；2) 线性+低秩双线性动作项（全局+状态相关控制）；3) 随机状态调制（局部校正）；4) 训练：后验条件EMA教师目标 + 一步一致性、多步rollout、开环观测预测损失；5) 推导多步rollout误差界，分离谱骨干/双线性交互放大与随机状态不匹配/建模残差的加性效应。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite和UAV-LiDAR自主导航任务上，Koopman Dreamer提升了长时域潜rollout的稳定性，在依赖高质量多步想象的任务上取得更强的闭环控制性能。

Conclusion: 通过谱约束的Koopman动态核心设计，可有效缓解潜世界模型长rollout的误差累积问题，在误差衰减与长期信息保持之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Latent world models improve sample efficiency in continuous control by optimizing policies over imagined latent trajectories, but common neural transitions offer limited direct control over modal persistence and error accumulation in long rollouts. We propose Koopman Dreamer, a Dreamer-style world model with a spectrally constrained deterministic latent dynamics core. Its Koopman-inspired backbone uses two-dimensional rotation--scaling blocks with bounded radii to represent damping, rotation, and near-periodic modes. Linear and low-rank bilinear action terms capture global and state-dependent control effects, while stochastic-state modulation supplies local correction information. To reduce the mismatch between posterior-conditioned training and prior-only imagination, the model combines posterior-conditioned EMA teacher targets with one-step consistency, multi-step rollout, and open-loop observation-prediction objectives. We further derive a multi-step rollout-error bound that separates amplification by the spectral backbone and bilinear interaction from the additive effects of stochastic-state mismatch and modeling residuals, clarifying the trade-off between error attenuation and long-term information retention. Experimental results on proprioceptive continuous-control tasks from the DeepMind Control Suite and UAV-LiDAR autonomous navigation demonstrate that Koopman Dreamer improves the stability of long-horizon latent rollouts and achieves stronger closed-loop control performance on tasks that rely on high-quality multi-step imagination.

</details>


### [162] [Analytic Distribution of Classifier-Free Guidance for Schedule Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19725)
*Enze Jiang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了无分类器引导（CFG）在扩散模型中的采样分布，推导了其概率流ODE的精确路径积分表示，揭示了CFG通过指数路径积分修正改变分布，并据此提出分布引导CFG（DG-CFG）调度策略，在Stable Diffusion 1.5上实现了更优的多样性-保真度权衡和采样效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFG的"乘积分布"启发式解释$p_0^\omega q_0^{1-\omega}$无法准确描述确定性引导动力学所采样的实际分布，缺乏对CFG诱导分布的精确理论刻画。

Method: 通过概率流ODE对CFG进行精确分析，推导了恒定引导和时间相关引导下的解析路径积分表示；基于此提出DG-CFG调度，该调度平衡各时间步贡献，同时考虑信号强度和低噪声区域的评分误差放大效应。

Result: 在具有解析评分的玩具模型上验证了理论预测的分布；在Stable Diffusion 1.5上，DG-CFG改进了生成质量，提供了更强的多样性-保真度权衡，尤其在强引导导致饱和和质量下降时效果明显；在固定图像质量目标下，DG-CFG用更少的采样步数达到目标指标，降低了采样成本。

Conclusion: CFG通过路径积分修正改变分布，时间相关调度通过权重$\omega(t)-1$进入该修正；DG-CFG调度有效改善了强引导下的生成退化问题，提升了采样效率。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is the default mechanism for conditional generation in diffusion models, but the distribution sampled by its deterministic guided dynamics is not captured by the usual product-distribution heuristic $p_0^ωq_0^{1-ω}$. We analyze CFG through the probability flow ODE and derive exact analytic path-integral representations of the induced distributions for both constant and time-dependent guidance. The resulting formulas show that CFG modifies $p_{t_0}$ by an exponential path-integral correction, and that a time-dependent schedule enters this correction through the weight $ω(t)-1$. This characterization explains how score discrepancies accumulate along sampling trajectories and motivates Distribution-Guided CFG (DG-CFG), a schedule that balances timestep contributions while accounting for signal strength and low-noise score-error amplification. A toy model with analytic scores closely verifies the predicted distributions. On Stable Diffusion~1.5, DG-CFG improves generation and yields a stronger diversity--fidelity trade-off across guidance strengths, with especially clear gains when strong guidance causes saturation and quality degradation in constant and heuristic schedules. Across NFE budgets, DG-CFG reaches fixed image-quality targets with fewer sampling steps, reducing the sampling cost needed to achieve target metrics.

</details>


### [163] [The World Model Remembers, the Actor Forgets: Dream Rehearsal for Continual Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19749)
*Gurp Nijjer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DreamV3在任务序列中训练时会出现灾难性遗忘。研究发现遗忘的是actor（行为策略）而非world model（世界模型）。通过冻结世界模型并使用基于世界模型自身梦境的分级自模仿（graded dream rehearsal），可以在无需任务标签、不增加参数的情况下实现持续学习，在4任务和8任务链上均保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中，虽然已有共识认为回放缓冲区可缓解遗忘，但从未系统测量过到底是哪个组件（world model还是actor）导致了遗忘。本文旨在通过组件级探针精确定位遗忘根源。

Method: 使用预注册的组件级探针（3个种子）测量DreamV3在任务序列上的遗忘模式；提出graded dream rehearsal方法：冻结world model，基于其对旧任务的梦境评分进行筛选，用监督式自模仿学习恢复actor技能。

Result: World model保留了对旧任务的奖励判别（保留比~1.0）、价值估计和终止结构，而actor的行为完全崩溃。标准想象强化学习无法恢复技能（0/3种子），而graded dream rehearsal在零环境交互下恢复3/3种子。在4任务链上3/3成功（plain replay为0/3），8任务链3/3成功，优于匹配的真实经验克隆（配对差+0.13，95% CI [0.07, 0.24]）。

Conclusion: 持续强化学习中的遗忘本质上是通道问题（actor通道失效）而非记忆问题（world model完好）。Graded dream rehearsal提供了一种无需任务标签、不增加参数的有效持续学习方案。梦境评分步骤是关键，提出的离线选择量规和实现优先评分规则可有效防止评分失败。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement-learning agents of the DreamerV3 family forget catastrophically when trained on task sequences, even when an unbounded replay buffer preserves every earlier experience. We ask a question the continual-RL literature has assumed an answer to but never measured: which component forgets? Under never-clear replay, pre-registered component-level probes (n=3 seeds throughout) show that the world model retains essentially everything measurable about old tasks -- reward discrimination (retention ratio ~1.0), value estimates, and termination structure -- while the actor's behavior collapses. Forgetting in this regime is a channel problem, not a memory problem. We demonstrate this by intervention: with the world model frozen and identical imagined rollouts, reinforcement learning in imagination fails to recover a lost skill (0/3 seeds), while supervised self-imitation on the world model's own graded dreams recovers it on 3/3 seeds with zero environment interaction. Interleaved during training, this graded dream rehearsal yields a task-label-free, parameter-constant continual learner: 3/3 four-task chains retained where plain replay passes 0/3, 3/3 eight-task chains, and consistent gains over matched real-episode cloning (paired difference +0.13, bootstrap 95% CI [0.07, 0.24], complete seed separation). The dream-grading step is load-bearing: we characterize two scoring failure modes, provide an offline selection gauge that caught both before they contaminated results, and give a realized-first grading rule that closes them. All experiments were pre-registered with committed protocols; every refuted hypothesis is reported.

</details>


### [164] [Convergence-Latency-Aware Adaptive Modulation and Resource Allocation in RIS-Assisted Wireless Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19759)
*Liwei Wang,Wen Chen,Jun Li,Qingqing Wu,Ming Ding,Xusheng Zhu,Qiong Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RIS辅助的无线联邦学习系统，通过自适应调制和子信道分配来优化收敛与延迟的权衡，提出了一种低复杂度的混合交替优化框架，实验证明该方法在复杂任务和挑战性无线场景下能实现更快的收敛和更高的测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线联邦学习在阻塞传播环境下因不可靠传输导致训练延迟大、收敛性能差，现有研究缺乏对调制依赖传输错误下学习收敛与通信延迟之间权衡的刻画。

Method: 推导了符号错误率对FL损失衰减影响的收敛相关上界，将联合收敛-延迟优化问题建模为混合整数非线性规划问题，并采用低复杂度混合交替优化框架求解。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Speech Commands数据集上的实验表明，所提方案在收敛速度和测试精度上均优于现有自适应通信方案。

Conclusion: RIS辅助的自适应调制与子信道分配策略能有效提升无线联邦学习在复杂任务和挑战性无线场景下的性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) over wireless networks suffers from significant training latency and degraded convergence due to unreliable wireless transmission, especially under blocked propagation environments. Although reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) can improve communication reliability, existing wireless FL studies rarely characterize the trade-off between learning convergence and communication delay under modulation-dependent transmission errors. In this paper, we consider a wireless FL system operating under RIS-assisted blocked-link propagation scenarios, and focus on adaptive modulation and sub-channel allocation for convergence-latency aware communication design. By characterizing the effect of symbol errors on uploaded local gradients, we derive a convergence-related upper bound that reveals the impact of symbol error rate (SER) on FL loss decay. Based on this result, we formulate a joint convergence-latency optimization problem, which is cast as a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, and solve it using a low-complexity hybrid alternating optimization framework. Extensive experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Speech Commands show that the proposed scheme consistently achieves faster convergence and higher test accuracy than existing adaptive communication schemes, especially in complex tasks and challenging wireless scenarios.

</details>


### [165] [An Isotropy-Preserving Spectral Cap for Muon: Theory and Three Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19771)
*Jiachun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，基于损失在权重缩放下的近似尺度不变性假设，解释了Muon优化器为何导致权重矩阵的Frobenius和谱范数更快增长（t^{1/2} vs t^{1/4}），并提出一种轻量级“谱帽”方法——仅抑制更新中顶部奇异方向的一阶增长——以控制输出协方差而不冻结训练；在nanoGPT前馈投影、混合专家路由器和FlashAttention注意力头的实验中，谱帽提升了各向同性并防止了具体失败（如路由器坍缩到单一专家、注意力头近发散），而验证损失基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon及相关矩阵符号优化器在大语言模型预训练中日益广泛使用，但它们对单个权重矩阵内部几何结构的影响尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一理解空白。

Method: 基于损失在权重缩放下的近似尺度不变性假设，推导SGD与Muon更新步长的差异；提出“谱帽”方法，仅投影移除更新中顶部奇异方向的一阶增长；在nanoGPT前馈投影、64专家混合专家路由器、bf16 FlashAttention的query/key投影三个系统上进行实验验证。

Result: Muon的矩阵符号步骤移除了SGD中固有的1/||W||刹车，导致范数增长更快（t^{1/2} vs t^{1/4}）；谱帽在三个系统中均提升了各向同性，并在路由器坍缩和注意力头近发散等边缘场景阻止了具体失败，同时验证损失基本不变。

Conclusion: 谱帽能有效控制输出协方差W K_X W^T，在不冻结训练的前提下防止训练失败，但尺度不变性假设较强，目前结果仍是初步的。

Abstract: Muon and related matrix-sign optimizers are increasingly used to pre-train large language models, but their effect on the internal geometry of individual weight matrices is not well understood. This preliminary report proposes a unified framework built on a single idealizing assumption -- exact scale invariance of the loss under weight rescaling, which holds approximately in normalization-heavy networks. Under this assumption, plain SGD carries a built-in 1/||W|| brake on its update size, whereas Muon's matrix-sign step removes that brake, so both the Frobenius and spectral norms drift outward faster (t^{1/2} versus t^{1/4}). We further observe that the spectral-norm perturbation has a non-negative second-order term. This implies that a lightweight "spectral cap" -- which projects out only the first-order growth of the single top singular direction from each update -- can control the output covariance W K_X W^T without freezing training: the weight keeps learning through non-top directions, top-direction rotation, and top switching. We relate this cap to the min-entropy (H-infinity) of the singular-value spectrum. We then study three systems trained with Muon: a nanoGPT feed-forward projection, a 64-expert mixture-of-experts router, and the query/key projections of a bf16 FlashAttention block. In each case the cap increases isotropy and, at the margins -- a router collapsing to a single expert, and the near-divergence of one attention head -- prevents a concrete failure, while leaving validation loss essentially unchanged. We emphasize that the scale-invariance assumption is strong and that these small-scale results are preliminary; comments are welcome.

</details>


### [166] [OPIUM: Mitigating Steering Externalities and Over-Refusal via Dual Objective Latent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19806)
*Kavin Aravindan,Arihant Rastogi,Aadi Prasad,Krishak Aneja,Saiyam Jain,Vaishnavi Shivkumar,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPIUM方法，通过在激活空间中优化转向向量，在保持期望干预效果的同时匹配更安全的参考行为，从而缓解激活转向带来的安全-效用权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活转向在推理时控制大语言模型时会产生意外副作用：效用向量可能削弱安全行为，而拒绝向量可能对良性提示产生过度拒绝。需要一种方法来净化转向向量，缓解这种安全-效用权衡。

Method: OPIUM（通过效用流形优化保护注入）是一种无需训练的方法，通过表示匹配来净化转向向量。给定两个提示集上的参考行为，OPIUM优化一个新的转向向量，使其在保持期望干预引起的下游表示的同时，在原始向量失效的提示上匹配更安全的参考行为。

Result: 在转向外部性和过度拒绝两种场景下，OPIUM相对于原始转向和方向消融方法改善了安全-效用权衡，表明激活转向的有害副作用通常可以在激活空间中直接缓解。

Conclusion: OPIUM方法有效地在激活空间中直接缓解了激活转向带来的有害副作用，为平衡大语言模型的安全性和实用性提供了一种轻量级、无需训练的新方案。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight mechanism for controlling large language models at inference time, but steering vectors can have unintended externalities: utility vectors may weaken safety behavior, while refusal vectors may induce over-refusal on benign prompts. We introduce OPIUM (Optimizing Protected Injections via Utility Manifolds), a training-free method for sanitizing steering vectors through representation matching. Given reference behaviors on two prompt sets, OPIUM optimizes a new steering vector that preserves the downstream representations induced by the desired intervention while matching a safer reference behavior on prompts where the original vector fails. Across steering-externality and over-refusal settings, OPIUM improves the safety--utility tradeoff relative to vanilla steering and directional ablation, suggesting that harmful side effects of activation steering can often be mitigated directly in activation space.

</details>


### [167] [Auto-Fill: Learning to Predict Missing Values Accurately with Specialist Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19847)
*Yurong Liu,Yeye He,Haoyu Dong,Junjie Xing,Shi Han,Dongmei Zhang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-Fill方法，通过后训练三个专用小型语言模型（分别擅长世界知识、文本推理和代码推理），并结合校准集成机制（动态选择最自信的专家或弃权），以极低成本（不到前沿模型的1%）在表格缺失值预测任务上取得了优于o3-pro、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1等先进推理模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型虽然能通过跨行列推理预测表格缺失值，但部署成本高且易产生幻觉/假阳性预测，需要一种高精度、低成本的缺失值预测方案。

Method: 后训练三个小型语言模型分别专精于世界知识、文本推理和代码推理；设计校准集成机制，动态选择置信度最高的专家模型输出结果，否则选择弃权以保证高精度。

Result: 在11个基准、2200个真实表格上的实验表明，Auto-Fill的准确率优于o3-pro、Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1等前沿模型，且成本仅为它们的不到1%。

Conclusion: 专门化与校准弃权策略在表格数据领域效果显著，证明了以极低成本实现高精度缺失值预测的可行性。

Abstract: Predicting missing cell values in tabular data is a fundamental problem in data cleaning. While state-of-the-art reasoning models show great promise in predicting missing values in tables, by reasoning holistically across rows and columns, they are costly to deploy at scale and tend to be overconfident, often generating hallucinated or false-positive predictions.
  In this paper, we observe that achieving high-precision missing-value prediction in tables requires a distinct combination of three capabilities: (1) world knowledge, (2) text-based reasoning, and (3) code-based reasoning. We systematically explore design choices for combining these capabilities, and propose an Auto-Fill approach that post-trains three specialist small language models (SLMs), each optimized for one capability. We develop a calibrated ensemble mechanism that either dynamically selects the most confident specialist or abstains, ensuring high accuracy.
  Extensive experiments on 11 benchmarks with 2200 real tables drawn from diverse domains show that Auto-Fill achieves superior accuracy compared to state-of-the-art reasoning models (e.g., o3-pro, Gemini 3 Pro, and DeepSeek R1), while operating at a fraction (less than 1%) of the cost of these frontier models. Our results highlight the effectiveness of specialization and calibrated abstention in the important domain of tabular data. Auto-Fill is publicly available at https://github.com/lyrain2001/auto-fill.

</details>


### [168] [Asymptotically Optimal Regret for Reinforcement Learning without Horizon Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19854)
*Runlong Zhou,Zihan Zhang,Maryam Fazel,Simon S. Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域时间齐次表格型马尔可夫决策过程，提出了一种新的算法，实现了 $\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)$ 的遗憾上界，该界与 $H$ 无关且在渐近意义上最优，匹配了上下文赌博机的下界 $\Omega(\sqrt{SAK})$。主要技术贡献包括利用最优值函数关于 $h$ 的单调性、将值函数投影到 $S$ 维网格上、引入时间截断论证、设计保持乐观性和单调性的切割奖励，以及证明时间齐次MDP的总偏差新界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾界通常依赖 $\log H$ 因子或具有较差的 $S$ 依赖关系（如 $\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$），本文旨在实现完全 $H$-free 且渐近最优的遗憾界，消除 $\log H$ 依赖并大幅改善状态空间维度的多项式依赖。

Method: (1) 利用最优值函数 $V_h^*$ 关于 $h$ 的单调性进行union bound，避免 $\min\{\log H, S\}$ 因子；(2) 将值函数非平凡地投影到 $S$ 维网格上；(3) 引入时间截断（horizon-truncation）论证实现基于奖励的探索，移除单独奖励-free探索阶段；(4) 设计切割奖励（cutting bonus），同时保持乐观性和规划所需的单调性；(5) 证明时间齐次MDP的总偏差新界，控制切割奖励中的裁剪方差项，可调节 $S$ 的多项式依赖且不含 $H$ 依赖。

Result: 得到 $\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)$ 的遗憾上界，失败概率为 $\delta$，其中 $\tilde O$ 隐藏 $\mathsf{poly}\log(S,A,K,1/\delta)$ 因子。该界与 $H$ 无关，渐近匹配 $\Omega(\sqrt{SAK})$ 下界，完全消除了 Zhang et al. (2021) 中的 $\log H$ 依赖，并大幅改进了 Zhang et al. (2022) 的 $\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$ 界。

Conclusion: 本文首次在时间齐次表格型MDP中实现了渐近最优的 $H$-free 遗憾界，通过一系列技术贡献（单调性利用、网格投影、时间截断、切割奖励、总偏差新界）解决了时间非齐次最优值函数带来的技术困难，为后续研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: We study horizon-free regret minimization for finite-horizon time-homogeneous tabular Markov decision processes with $S$ states, $A$ actions, horizon $H$, and per-trajectory total reward bounded by $1$.
  We propose a new algorithm and prove a regret upper bound \[\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)\] with failure probability $δ$, where $K$ is the number of episodes and $\tilde O(\cdot)$ hides $\mathsf{poly}\log(S,A,K,1/δ)$.
  Thus, the regret is $H$-free and asymptotically optimal, matching the contextual-bandit lower bound $Ω(\sqrt{SAK})$ up to logarithmic factors. This completely removes the $\log H$ dependence from the previous $\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK\log H}+S^2A\log H)$ guarantee of Zhang et al. (2021), and drastically improves the prior best horizon-free regret $\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$ of Zhang et al. (2022) asymptotically.
  The main technical difficulty is that the optimal value functions $\{V_h^*\}_{h=1}^H$ are time-inhomogeneous even though the transition kernel is time-homogeneous. A direct union bound over all value functions typically incurs an additional $\min\{\log H,S\}$ factor. We avoid this factor by (i) exploiting the monotonicity of $V_h^*$ in $h$ and (ii) non-trivially projecting the value functions onto an $S$-dimensional grid.
  Our analysis relies on three additional ingredients. First, we introduce a horizon-truncation argument that enables reward-based exploration and removes the cost of a separate reward-free exploration phase. Second, we design a cutting bonus that preserves both optimism and the monotonicity needed for planning. Third, we prove a new bound on total deviation for time-homogeneous MDPs, which controls the clipped variance terms in the cutting bonus with adjustable polynomial dependence on $S$ and without any dependence on $H$. Together, these tools yield an asymptotically optimal horizon-free regret guarantee.

</details>


### [169] [Adversarial Frontiers: Minimum-Norm Attack Ensembles for Robustness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19855)
*Luca Scionis,Luca Melis,Maura Pintor,Fabio Brau,Ambra Demontis,Giorgio Fumera,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对当前对抗性鲁棒性评估中固定攻击预算、单一范数、攻击集合缺乏最优性保证等局限，提出一个统一评估框架：基于最小范数攻击和跨多种范数的鲁棒性-扰动曲线，定义攻击前沿和防御前沿，将评估形式化为前沿逼近问题，通过可控查询预算构建最优攻击子集，并引入防御最优性指数（DOI）实现无需参考ε的模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性评估依赖预设攻击集成（如AutoAttack），存在三个根本局限：1）单一扰动预算ε下的排名不稳定，不同模型的鲁棒性-扰动曲线可能交叉或衰减速度不同；2）预设攻击集成无法提供最优性证据，与最坏情况性能之间存在未知差距；3）固定攻击配置无法系统控制攻击强度与评估成本之间的权衡。

Method: 1）构建一个综合的最小范数攻击池，覆盖ℓ₀, ℓ₁, ℓ₂, ℓ∞四种范数，生成鲁棒性-扰动曲线；2）定义攻击前沿为攻击池对模型产生的最坏情况鲁棒性估计；3）将评估形式化为前沿逼近问题，通过可控查询预算构建最小范数攻击集成（优化的攻击子集），预算越大估计越紧致；4）定义防御前沿为给定扰动大小下模型集合中的最大鲁棒性；5）提出防御最优性指数（DOI）用于衡量各防御与防御前沿的差距。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上，所提出的攻击集成在每个预算层级上要么匹配要么超越AutoAttack，且查询成本固定可控，为从业者提供了可控查询、基于曲线而非固定ε的评估替代方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架克服了固定ε评估的固有缺陷，通过前沿逼近和可控查询预算实现了更可靠、更全面的对抗鲁棒性评估，DOI指标无需选择参考ε即可进行模型排名，提升了评估的稳定性和公平性。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness is commonly evaluated with predefined attack ensembles, such as AutoAttack, at a single perturbation budget $\varepsilon$ and on a selective choice of perturbation norms. We argue this formulation is fundamentally limited. First, robustness--perturbation curves may intersect or decay at different rates across models, making single-$\varepsilon$ rankings unstable. Second, current ensembles provide no evidence of optimality, leaving an unknown gap to worst-case performance. Third, fixed attack configurations provide no systematic control over the trade-off between attack strength and evaluation cost. To address these limitations, we introduce a unified evaluation framework based on a comprehensive pool of minimum-norm attacks and robustness--perturbation curves across $\ell_0$, $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$ and $\ell_\infty$ norms. We define the attack frontier as the worst-case robustness estimate the attack pool produces against a model. We then formalize evaluation as a frontier-approximation problem, constructing minimum-norm attack ensembles, optimized subsets of the comprehensive pool, that approach the frontier under a controllable query budget, with larger budgets monotonically tightening the estimate. Furthermore, we define the defense frontier as the maximum robustness across the model set at each perturbation size. We finally propose the Defense Optimality Index to rank defenses by their gap to the defense frontier, providing a ranking without selecting a reference $\varepsilon$. On CIFAR-10 and ImageNet, our ensembles match or exceed AutoAttack on most defenses at every budget tier, at fixed and controllable query cost, offering practitioners a query-controlled, curve-based alternative to fixed-$\varepsilon$ evaluation.

</details>


### [170] [Local Causal Structure Learning in the Presence of Latent Variables and Selection Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19866)
*Zheng Li,Hao Zhang,Ruxin Wang,Ruichu Cai,Kun Zhang,Feng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoCaLS算法，能够在存在潜在变量和选择偏差的情况下，从观测数据中高效识别目标变量的直接因果邻居，无需重建全局因果结构，且计算成本远低于全局方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法要么学习全局结构导致计算开销大，要么假设不存在潜在变量和选择偏差，这些假设在现实场景中常被违反。因此，本文研究存在潜在变量和选择偏差时的局部因果结构学习问题。

Method: 首先刻画一个无需恢复全局结构即可进行目标特异性因果发现的局部区域；然后在观测分布与全局因果结构之间建立理论桥梁；基于此提出LoCaLS算法，在标准假设下保证正确性与完备性，能识别与全局方法相同的直接因果。

Result: 在随机和真实结构上的实验表明，LoCaLS在结构准确性上持续优于现有局部方法，同时计算量远小于全局方法。在基因表达数据集上的应用验证了其生物学合理性。

Conclusion: LoCaLS在允许潜在变量和选择偏差的条件下，实现了高效且准确的局部因果结构学习，在大规模生物数据分析中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Discovering the direct causes and effects of a target variable from observational data is a fundamental problem in causal discovery, with broad applications in domains such as gene regulatory analysis and biomedical research. Existing causal discovery methods either learn a global causal structure, which incurs substantial computational cost, or assume the absence of latent variables and selection bias, assumptions that are often violated in real-world settings. Motivated by these challenges, we study local causal structure learning in the presence of latent variables and selection bias. Specifically, we first characterize a local region that enables target-specific causal discovery without recovering the entire global structure. We then establish a theoretical bridge between causal information learned from the observed distribution induced on this local region and the corresponding information in the global causal structure. Building on these foundations, we propose LoCaLS, a local causal structure learning algorithm that is sound and complete under standard assumptions and identifies the same direct causes and effects of a target variable as those identifiable by global causal discovery methods, while allowing for latent variables and selection bias. Extensive experiments on random and real-world structures demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves higher structural accuracy than existing local methods while requiring substantially less computational effort than state-of-the-art global methods. Furthermore, applications to two real-world gene expression datasets reveal biologically plausible target-specific causal structures, demonstrating its practical applicability in large-scale biological data analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Nonlinear Bias-Compensated Adaptive Filter and Its Application for Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19902)
*Yi Peng,Haiquan Zhao,Jinhui Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有非线性自适应滤波算法忽略输入噪声、以及偏置补偿核最小均方算法存在字典固定且鲁棒性差的问题，本文提出了RFFBCGA算法，该算法利用随机傅里叶特征保持固定网络结构并补偿输入噪声，同时通过广义自适应函数增强对各种噪声场景的鲁棒性，仿真实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性自适应滤波算法大多仅考虑输出噪声而忽略输入噪声；BCKLMS算法虽能处理输入噪声，但存在两大局限：固定大小字典无法充分捕获输入信号特征，且基于LMS的算法在非高斯输出噪声下鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出RFFBCGA算法：基于随机傅里叶特征偏置补偿（RFFBC）框架，既保持固定网络结构又通过BC项有效缓解输入噪声干扰；同时利用广义自适应（GA）函数的灵活形式增强算法在不同噪声场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 大量仿真实验（包括真实时间序列预测任务）表明所提方法优于现有算法。

Conclusion: RFFBCGA算法有效解决了输入噪声干扰和非高斯输出噪声下的鲁棒性问题，在保持固定网络结构的同时改善了输入信号表征能力。

Abstract: Most existing nonlinear adaptive filtering algorithms only account for output noise, neglecting the fact that input noise is also prevalent in practice. Although the recently proposed bias-compensated kernel least mean square (BCKLMS) algorithm addresses input noise in the nonlinear errors-in-variables (EIV) model, it still suffers from two major limitations. First, the use of a fixed-size dictionary restricts network growth but also prevents it from fully capturing the characteristics of the input signal. Second, as an least mean square (LMS) based algorithm, it exhibits poor robustness in the presence of non-Gaussian noise in the output signal. To overcome these issues, this paper proposes the random Fourier bias-compensated filter under general adaptive function (RFFBCGA) algorithm. Within the random Fourier feature based bias-compensated (RFFBC) framework, the proposed algorithm not only maintains a fixed network structure and effectively mitigates input noise interference through the BC term, but also achieves improved characterization of the input signal. Moreover, by leveraging the flexible form of the general adaptive (GA) function, the algorithm's robustness across various noise scenarios is further enhanced. Extensive simulations, including real-world time series prediction tasks, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [172] [Time Series Network Utilization KPI Forecasting Using Advanced AI/ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19974)
*Niraj Gadhe,Kirti Bhardwaj,Moulik Jain,Shubhi Sharma,Vinay Saini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种时间序列预测模型（包括季节性分解、Prophet、随机森林、XGBoost、SVR以及双向LSTM和卷积LSTM等深度学习架构）在网络带宽利用率预测中的表现，使用MAPE、NRMSE和R平方指标在通用接口数据集上进行基准测试，为网络容量规划提供了准确性与计算效率之间的权衡见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用、云基础设施和物联网生态系统的快速扩张使得主动式资源调配变得至关重要。传统反应式方法无法准确预测流量波动，导致过度配置、意外停机和服务质量下降，直接影响运营预算和业务连续性。

Method: 在通用接口数据集上，评估了季节性分解、Prophet、随机森林、XGBoost、支持向量回归（SVR）以及双向LSTM和卷积LSTM等深度学习模型，采用MAPE、NRMSE和R平方指标进行基准测试。

Result: 研究提供了不同模型在准确性与计算效率之间的量化权衡结果，帮助识别适用于不同基础设施需求的最优预测模型。

Conclusion: 该研究为网络工程师、运维人员和业务所有者提供了可操作的见解，使他们能够根据具体的网络基础设施需求选择最优的带宽利用率预测模型，从而实现高效容量规划。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of data-intensive applications, cloud infrastructure, and IoT ecosystems has made proactive resource provisioning critical for maintaining optimal network performance. However, network administrators face a constant battle against capacity constraints, where traditional reactive approaches fail to accurately anticipate traffic fluctuations. This inability to foresee demand leads to costly over-provisioning, unexpected downtime, and degraded quality of service directly impacting operational budgets and business continuity. To achieve efficient capacity planning, accurate forecasting of bandwidth utilization is essential. This study addresses the challenge by evaluating a diverse spectrum of models including seasonal decomposition, Prophet, Random Forest, XGBoost, Support Vector Regression, and advanced deep learning architectures like bidirectional and Convolutional LSTMs - using a common interface dataset benchmarked across MAPE, NRMSE, and R-square metrics. Ultimately, this research delivers actionable insights into the trade-offs between model accuracy and computational efficiency, empowering engineers, operators, and business owners to select the optimal forecasting model for their specific infrastructure needs.

</details>


### [173] [Good Practice Guide for quantifying uncertainties for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19999)
*P. Harris,C. Bench,M. Rinkevičius,V. Marozas,L. Coquelin,A. Thompson,M. Nandi,U. Hackstein,P. J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是QUMPHY项目的实践指南，系统介绍了面向光电容积描记（PPG）信号机器学习模型的不确定性量化方法，涵盖模型选择、回归/分类任务、不确定性量化技术实现与验证、六个基准问题及数据集、配套软件工具及伦理考量，最终给出总结与建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对可穿戴设备PPG信号处理中机器学习模型的不确定性量化问题，目前缺乏系统性实践指导，QUMPHY项目旨在填补这一空白，为从业者提供从模型选择到不确定性量化实施的完整指南。

Method: 采用综述性指南撰写方式：1）对比不同机器学习模型在回归与分类任务上的表现；2）分类介绍模型依赖与模型无关的不确定性量化方法及其实现；3）设计六个基准问题并给出对应数据集；4）开发辅助软件工具；5）纳入伦理问题讨论。

Result: 形成了涵盖模型对比、不确定性量化方法实施指南、基准测试体系（六个问题+数据集）、配套软件和伦理考量的一整套实践指导框架。

Conclusion: 该指南为PPG信号机器学习实践提供了从模型选型、不确定性量化到验证的全流程建议，有助于提升可穿戴设备信号处理的可信度与可靠性。

Abstract: This Good Practice Guide presents work done in the QUMPHY project (Uncertainty quantification for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals) that considered both machine learning and uncertainty quantification for problems which used photoplethysmography (PPG) signals from wearable devices as input. It provides high-level guidance on what types of machine learning model might be used and how different models compare when applied to both regression and classification tasks. It provides guidance on the implementation of different methods for uncertainty quantification, covering both model-dependent and model-independent techniques, and on the validation of the results provided by those methods. It also describes six benchmark problems together with pointers to different benchmark datasets for each problem. Software is described that can assist practitioners in implementing the methods described herein and there is a brief consideration of ethical issues. It concludes with a summary and recommendations.

</details>


### [174] [The Blessing of Dimensionality: How Near-Orthogonality in High-Dimensional Spaces Explains Temporal Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20301)
*Abigail Woodring,Adrian Chan,Rana Muhammad Shahroz Khan,Sukwon Yun,Chau-Wai Wong,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了PortLLM方法在持续预训练中的长期时间可移植性，并通过理论和实证分析证明其在10个持续预训练步骤后仍保持有效性，高维向量的近正交性是关键原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前PortLLM仅展示了LoRA补丁的短期时间可移植性，其长期性能以及理论有效性尚不清楚。

Method: (1) 在Mistral、Gemma、Qwen三个基础模型上进行了10个持续预训练步骤的长期实证研究；(2) 提供了两种理论分析解释PortLLM的有效性。

Result: 长期可移植性在持续预训练中持续存在，无需在基础模型更新时重复微调；高维向量的近正交性是时间可移植性的关键理论依据。

Conclusion: PortLLM在长期持续预训练场景下仍然有效，近正交性为其有效性提供了理论支持，损失景观的几何视角有助于比较不同适配方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has been widely used to adapt large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks. Parameter efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are frequently used to reduce computational costs. PortLLM is a training-free and data-free scheme used to adapt LLMs after continual pretraining. Although the initial PortLLM results show that LoRA patches exhibit short-term temporal portability, the long-term performance of PortLLM across several updates of continual pretraining remains underexplored. Furthermore, the intriguing effectiveness of PortLLM is not well understood from a theoretical standpoint. We address these two open questions by (1) performing an extensive empirical study of the long-term temporal portability of PortLLM patches across 10 continual pretraining steps using base models Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen; and (2) offering two theoretical analyses to explain our observation that the simple PortLLM method achieves competitive performance. We find empirically that the portability persists across longer time duration, indicating that repeated fine-tuning is not required when the base model is periodically updated. We find theoretically that near-orthogonality of high-dimensional vectors is a key justification for temporal portability. Our analyses also demonstrate a geometric perspective of the loss landscape in facilitating the theoretical comparison of different adaptation options.

</details>


### [175] [Post-Training in Time Series Foundation Models: A Unifying Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20002)
*Shifeng Xie,Ambroise Odonnat,Zehao Xiao,Lei Zan,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan,Themis Palpanas,Boris N. Oreshkin,Chenghao Liu,Keli Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）的后训练方法，根据预测流水线中的干预位置将其分为五类：参数适配、上下文增强、模型组合、输出处理与不确定性控制、压缩与专业化，并讨论了各类方法的现状与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSFMs虽已成为通用时间序列分析模型，但仅靠预训练不足以可靠地部署到下游任务，面临领域偏移、任务异质性、监督有限和计算约束等挑战，因此需要后训练方法来弥合预训练模型与下游部署之间的差距。
